Implement the following plan:

# Fix: Per-session agent resolution in multi-session checkpoints

## Context

A checkpoint can contain multiple sessions from different agents (e.g., session 0 from Claude, session 1 from Gemini). The per-session agent type **is stored correctly** in each session's `metadata.json` on `entire/checkpoints/v1` (`CommittedMetadata.Agent`), but the consumption layer collapses everything to a single agent — always session 0's. This means session 1's transcript gets written to the wrong directory, with the wrong file extension, and the wrong resume command.

The user's key insight: `RestoreLogsOnly()` already reads each session's metadata (including `content.Metadata.Agent`) inside its loop — it just ignores it. Rather than threading `SessionAgents` arrays through `CheckpointInfo` → `RewindPoint` → consumers, we resolve per-session agents **at consumption time** inside `RestoreLogsOnly()` and return the per-session info so callers can print correct resume commands.

## Changes

### 1. Add `RestoredSession` struct

**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — New struct returned by `RestoreLogsOnly`:
```go
type RestoredSession struct {
    SessionID string
    Agent     agent.AgentType
    Prompt    string
}
```

### 2. Change `RestoreLogsOnly` return type

**`strategy/strategy.go`** — `LogsOnlyRestorer` interface (line 473-478):
```go
type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
    RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error)
}
```

**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Implementation signature (line 619):
```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error)
```

### 3. Per-session agent resolution in `RestoreLogsOnly()`

**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Inside the session loop (after line 704), use `content.Metadata.Agent` to resolve a per-session agent:

```go
// Resolve per-session agent (fall back to outer ag for old checkpoints)
sessionAgent := ag
sessionAgentDir := sessionDir
if content.Metadata.Agent != "" {
    if perAg, err := ResolveAgentForRewind(content.Metadata.Agent); err == nil {
        sessionAgent = perAg
        if d, err := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot); err == nil {
            sessionAgentDir = d
        }
    }
}

sessionFile := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)
```

Collect `RestoredSession` entries and return them.

Remove the outer single-agent `sessionDir` setup (lines 655-663) — each session computes its own dir.

### 4. Per-session agent in `classifySessionsForRestore()`

**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Same per-session resolution pattern:

- Add `repoRoot string` parameter (caller at line 667 already has `repoRoot`)
- Inside the loop, resolve per-session agent from `content.Metadata.Agent`
- Use per-session agent + dir for `ResolveSessionFilePath`

### 5. Update all callers in `rewind.go`

5 call sites (lines 556, 605, 887, 928, 998) currently discard the return:
```go
if err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true); err != nil {
```

Change to capture `[]RestoredSession`:
```go
sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true)
if err != nil {
```

Pass `sessions` to `printMultiSessionResumeCommands` (see step 7).

### 6. Simplify `resume.go` caller

**`resume.go`** (lines 408-486) currently:
1. Calls `RestoreLogsOnly(point, force)`
2. Re-opens the repository and re-reads the metadata tree to print resume commands

With the new return type, eliminate the re-read — use `[]RestoredSession` directly:
```go
sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, force)
if err != nil { ... }
// Use sessions to print resume commands — no re-read needed
```

This removes ~40 lines of fallback/re-read logic (lines 422-486).

### 7. Update `printMultiSessionResumeCommands()`

**`rewind.go`** — Change signature from `(point RewindPoint, agent Agent)` to `(sessions []strategy.RestoredSession)`:

For each session, resolve the agent from `session.Agent` via `ResolveAgentForRewind()`, then call `FormatResumeCommand`. Falls back to default agent for empty agent type.

### 8. Unify `getAgent()` with `ResolveAgentForRewind()`

**`rewind.go`** — Replace `getAgent()` body (line 33-42) to delegate:
```go
func getAgent(agentType agentpkg.AgentType) (agentpkg.Agent, error) {
    return strategy.ResolveAgentForRewind(agentType)
}
```

Fixes the inconsistency where `getAgent("")` errors but `ResolveAgentForRewind("")` falls back to Claude.

## Files to modify

| File | What |
|------|------|
| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go` | Change `LogsOnlyRestorer` interface return type |
| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Add `RestoredSession`, per-session agent in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/rewind.go` | Update 5 callers, rewrite `printMultiSessionResumeCommands()`, unify `getAgent()` |
| `cmd/entire/cli/resume.go` | Simplify caller to use returned `[]RestoredSession` |

2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`
3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`
4. Manual: create a checkpoint with two sessions from different agents, rewind, verify each transcript lands in the correct agent directory with correct resume commands

    26→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points.
    27→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListTemporaryCheckpoints for reading from shadow branches.
    28→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
    29→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	// Get checkpoint store
    35→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	// Get current HEAD to find matching shadow branch
    41→	head, err := repo.Head()
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Find sessions for current HEAD
    47→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(head.Hash().String())
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		// Log error but continue to check for logs-only points
    50→		sessions = nil
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	var allPoints []RewindPoint
    54→
    55→	// Collect checkpoint points from active sessions using checkpoint.GitStore
    56→	// Cache session prompts by session ID to avoid re-reading the same prompt file
    57→	sessionPrompts := make(map[string]string)
    58→
    59→	for _, state := range sessions {
    60→		checkpoints, err := store.ListTemporaryCheckpoints(context.Background(), state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID, state.SessionID, limit)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue // Error reading checkpoints, skip this session
    63→		}
    64→
    65→		for _, cp := range checkpoints {
    66→			// Get session prompt (cached by session ID)
    67→			sessionPrompt, ok := sessionPrompts[cp.SessionID]
    68→			if !ok {
    69→				sessionPrompt = readSessionPrompt(repo, cp.CommitHash, cp.MetadataDir)
    70→				sessionPrompts[cp.SessionID] = sessionPrompt
    71→			}
    72→
    73→			allPoints = append(allPoints, RewindPoint{
    74→				ID:               cp.CommitHash.String(),
    75→				Message:          cp.Message,
    76→				MetadataDir:      cp.MetadataDir,
    77→				Date:             cp.Timestamp,
    78→				IsTaskCheckpoint: cp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
    79→				ToolUseID:        cp.ToolUseID,
    80→				SessionID:        cp.SessionID,
    81→				SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
    82→				Agent:            state.AgentType,
    83→			})
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Sort by date, most recent first
    88→	sort.Slice(allPoints, func(i, j int) bool {
    89→		return allPoints[i].Date.After(allPoints[j].Date)
    90→	})
    91→
    92→	if len(allPoints) > limit {
    93→		allPoints = allPoints[:limit]
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Also include logs-only points from commit history
    97→	logsOnlyPoints, err := s.GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints(limit)
    98→	if err == nil && len(logsOnlyPoints) > 0 {
    99→		// Build set of existing point IDs for deduplication
   100→		existingIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   101→		for _, p := range allPoints {
   102→			existingIDs[p.ID] = true
   103→		}
   104→
   105→		// Add logs-only points that aren't already in the list
   106→		for _, p := range logsOnlyPoints {
   107→			if !existingIDs[p.ID] {
   108→				allPoints = append(allPoints, p)
   109→			}
   110→		}
   111→
   112→		// Re-sort by date
   113→		sort.Slice(allPoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   114→			return allPoints[i].Date.After(allPoints[j].Date)
   115→		})
   116→
   117→		// Re-trim to limit
   118→		if len(allPoints) > limit {
   119→			allPoints = allPoints[:limit]
   120→		}
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	return allPoints, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints finds commits in the current branch's history that have
   127→// condensed session logs on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch. These are commits that
   128→// were created with session data but the shadow branch has been condensed.
   129→//
   130→// The function works by:
   131→// 1. Getting all checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   132→// 2. Building a map of checkpoint ID -> checkpoint info
   133→// 3. Scanning the current branch history for commits with Entire-Checkpoint trailers
   134→// 4. Matching by checkpoint ID (stable across amend/rebase)
   135→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
   136→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   137→	if err != nil {
   138→		return nil, err
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Get all checkpoints from entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   142→	checkpoints, err := s.listCheckpoints()
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		// No checkpoints yet is fine
   145→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no checkpoints exist
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if len(checkpoints) == 0 {
   149→		return nil, nil
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	// Build map of checkpoint ID -> checkpoint info
   153→	// Checkpoint ID is the stable link from Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   154→	checkpointInfoMap := make(map[id.CheckpointID]CheckpointInfo)
   155→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   156→		if !cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   157→			checkpointInfoMap[cp.CheckpointID] = cp
   158→		}
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	// Get metadata branch tree for reading session prompts (best-effort, ignore errors)
   162→	metadataTree, _ := GetMetadataBranchTree(repo) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort for session prompts
   163→
   164→	head, err := repo.Head()
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Use LogOptions with Order=LogOrderCommitterTime to traverse all parents of merge commits.
   170→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   171→		From:  head.Hash(),
   172→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   173→	})
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	var points []RewindPoint
   179→	count := 0
   180→
   181→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   182→		if count >= logsOnlyScanLimit {
   183→			return errStop
   184→		}
   185→		count++
   186→
   187→		// Extract checkpoint ID from Entire-Checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   188→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   189→		if !found {
   190→			return nil
   191→		}
   192→		// Check if this checkpoint ID has metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1
   193→		cpInfo, found := checkpointInfoMap[cpID]
   194→		if !found {
   195→			return nil
   196→		}
   197→
   198→		// Create logs-only rewind point
   199→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   200→
   201→		// Read session prompts from metadata tree
   202→		var sessionPrompt string
   203→		var sessionPrompts []string
   204→		if metadataTree != nil {
   205→			checkpointPath := paths.CheckpointPath(cpInfo.CheckpointID) //nolint:staticcheck // already present in codebase
   206→			// For multi-session checkpoints, read all prompts
   207→			if cpInfo.SessionCount > 1 && len(cpInfo.SessionIDs) > 1 {
   208→				sessionPrompts = ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath, cpInfo.SessionCount, cpInfo.SessionIDs)
   209→				// Use the last (most recent) prompt as the main session prompt
   210→				if len(sessionPrompts) > 0 {
   211→					sessionPrompt = sessionPrompts[len(sessionPrompts)-1]
   212→				}
   213→			} else {
   214→				sessionPrompt = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   215→				if sessionPrompt != "" {
   216→					sessionPrompts = []string{sessionPrompt}
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→
   221→		points = append(points, RewindPoint{
   222→			ID:             c.Hash.String(),
   223→			Message:        message,
   224→			Date:           c.Author.When,
   225→			IsLogsOnly:     true,
   226→			CheckpointID:   cpInfo.CheckpointID,
   227→			Agent:          cpInfo.Agent,
   228→			SessionID:      cpInfo.SessionID,
   229→			SessionPrompt:  sessionPrompt,
   230→			SessionCount:   cpInfo.SessionCount,
   231→			SessionIDs:     cpInfo.SessionIDs,
   232→			SessionPrompts: sessionPrompts,
   233→		})
   234→
   235→		return nil
   236→	})
   237→
   238→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   239→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("error iterating commits: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	if len(points) > limit {
   243→		points = points[:limit]
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return points, nil
   247→}
   248→
   249→// Rewind restores the working directory to a checkpoint.
   250→//
   251→
   252→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
   253→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   254→	if err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Get the checkpoint commit
   259→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   260→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   266→	if err != nil {
   267→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	// Reset the shadow branch to the rewound checkpoint
   271→	// This ensures the next checkpoint will only include prompts from this point forward
   272→	if err := s.resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint(repo, commit); err != nil {
   273→		// Log warning but don't fail - file restoration is the primary operation
   274→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to reset shadow branch: %v\n", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Load session state to get untracked files that existed at session start
   278→	sessionID, hasSessionTrailer := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   279→	var preservedUntrackedFiles map[string]bool
   280→	if hasSessionTrailer {
   281→		state, stateErr := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   282→		if stateErr == nil && state != nil && len(state.UntrackedFilesAtStart) > 0 {
   283→			preservedUntrackedFiles = make(map[string]bool)
   284→			for _, f := range state.UntrackedFilesAtStart {
   285→				preservedUntrackedFiles[f] = true
   286→			}
   287→		}
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Build set of files in the checkpoint tree (excluding metadata)
   291→	checkpointFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   292→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   293→		if !strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   294→			checkpointFiles[f.Name] = true
   295→		}
   296→		return nil
   297→	})
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoint files: %w", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// Get HEAD tree to identify tracked files
   303→	head, err := repo.Head()
   304→	if err != nil {
   305→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   306→	}
   307→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD tree: %w", err)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	// Build set of files tracked in HEAD
   317→	trackedFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   318→	//nolint:errcheck // Error is not critical for rewind
   319→	_ = headTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   320→		trackedFiles[f.Name] = true
   321→		return nil
   322→	})
   323→
   324→	// Get repository root to walk from there
   325→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   326→	if err != nil {
   327→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to current directory
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	// Find and delete untracked files that aren't in the checkpoint
   331→	// These are likely files created by the agent in later checkpoints
   332→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
   333→		if walkErr != nil {
   334→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		// Get path relative to repo root
   338→		relPath, relErr := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
   339→		if relErr != nil {
   340→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Skip directories and protected paths
   344→		if info.IsDir() {
   345→			if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   346→				return filepath.SkipDir
   347→			}
   348→			return nil
   349→		}
   350→
   351→		// Skip files in protected directories
   352→		if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   353→			return nil
   354→		}
   355→
   356→		// If file is in checkpoint, it will be restored
   357→		if checkpointFiles[relPath] {
   358→			return nil
   359→		}
   360→
   361→		// If file is tracked in HEAD, don't delete (user's committed work)
   362→		if trackedFiles[relPath] {
   363→			return nil
   364→		}
   365→
   366→		// If file existed at session start, preserve it (untracked user files)
   367→		if preservedUntrackedFiles[relPath] {
   368→			return nil
   369→		}
   370→
   371→		// File is untracked and not in checkpoint - delete it (use absolute path)
   372→		if removeErr := os.Remove(path); removeErr == nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Deleted: %s\n", relPath)
   374→		}
   375→
   376→		return nil
   377→	})
   378→	if err != nil {
   379→		// Non-fatal - continue with restoration
   380→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: error walking directory: %v\n", err)
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	// Restore files from checkpoint
   384→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   385→		// Skip metadata directories - these are for checkpoint storage, not working dir
   386→		if strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   387→			return nil
   388→		}
   389→
   390→		contents, err := f.Contents()
   391→		if err != nil {
   392→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to read file %s: %w", f.Name, err)
   393→		}
   394→
   395→		// Ensure directory exists
   396→		dir := filepath.Dir(f.Name)
   397→		if dir != "." {
   398→			//nolint:gosec // G301: Need 0o755 for user directories during rewind
   399→			if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   400→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory %s: %w", dir, err)
   401→			}
   402→		}
   403→
   404→		// Write file with appropriate permissions
   405→		var perm os.FileMode = 0o644
   406→		if f.Mode == filemode.Executable {
   407→			perm = 0o755
   408→		}
   409→		if err := os.WriteFile(f.Name, []byte(contents), perm); err != nil {
   410→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write file %s: %w", f.Name, err)
   411→		}
   412→
   413→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Restored: %s\n", f.Name)
   414→		return nil
   415→	})
   416→	if err != nil {
   417→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate tree files: %w", err)
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	fmt.Println()
   421→	if len(point.ID) >= 7 {
   422→		fmt.Printf("Restored files from shadow commit %s\n", point.ID[:7])
   423→	} else {
   424→		fmt.Printf("Restored files from shadow commit %s\n", point.ID)
   425→	}
   426→	fmt.Println()
   427→
   428→	return nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint resets the shadow branch HEAD to the given checkpoint.
   432→// This ensures that when the user commits after rewinding, the next checkpoint will only
   433→// include prompts from the rewound point, not prompts from later checkpoints.
   434→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint(repo *git.Repository, commit *object.Commit) error {
   435→	// Extract session ID from the checkpoint commit's Entire-Session trailer
   436→	sessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   437→	if !found {
   438→		return errors.New("checkpoint has no Entire-Session trailer")
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Load session state to get the shadow branch name
   442→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→	if state == nil {
   447→		return fmt.Errorf("session %s not found", sessionID)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	// Reset the shadow branch to the checkpoint commit
   451→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   452→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   453→
   454→	// Update the reference to point to the checkpoint commit
   455→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commit.Hash)
   456→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   457→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update shadow branch: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Reset shadow branch %s to checkpoint %s\n", shadowBranchName, commit.Hash.String()[:7])
   461→	return nil
   462→}
   463→
   464→// CanRewind checks if rewinding is possible.
   465→// For manual-commit strategy, rewind restores files from a checkpoint - uncommitted changes are expected
   466→// and will be replaced by the checkpoint contents. Returns true with a warning message showing
   467→// what changes will be reverted.
   468→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   469→	return checkCanRewindWithWarning()
   470→}
   471→
   472→// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   473→// This allows showing warnings about untracked files that will be deleted.
   474→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   475→	// Logs-only points don't modify the working directory
   476→	if point.IsLogsOnly {
   477→		return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   481→	if err != nil {
   482→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Get the checkpoint commit
   486→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   487→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   488→	if err != nil {
   489→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   493→	if err != nil {
   494→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	// Load session state to get untracked files that existed at session start
   498→	sessionID, hasSessionTrailer := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   499→	var preservedUntrackedFiles map[string]bool
   500→	if hasSessionTrailer {
   501→		state, stateErr := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   502→		if stateErr == nil && state != nil && len(state.UntrackedFilesAtStart) > 0 {
   503→			preservedUntrackedFiles = make(map[string]bool)
   504→			for _, f := range state.UntrackedFilesAtStart {
   505→				preservedUntrackedFiles[f] = true
   506→			}
   507→		}
   508→	}
   509→
   510→	// Build set of files in the checkpoint tree (excluding metadata)
   511→	checkpointFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   512→	var filesToRestore []string
   513→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   514→		if !strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   515→			checkpointFiles[f.Name] = true
   516→			filesToRestore = append(filesToRestore, f.Name)
   517→		}
   518→		return nil
   519→	})
   520→	if err != nil {
   521→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoint files: %w", err)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Get HEAD tree to identify tracked files
   525→	head, err := repo.Head()
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   530→	if err != nil {
   531→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   532→	}
   533→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   534→	if err != nil {
   535→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD tree: %w", err)
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	// Build set of files tracked in HEAD
   539→	trackedFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   540→	//nolint:errcheck // Error is not critical for preview
   541→	_ = headTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   542→		trackedFiles[f.Name] = true
   543→		return nil
   544→	})
   545→
   546→	// Get repository root to walk from there
   547→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   548→	if err != nil {
   549→		repoRoot = "."
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Find untracked files that would be deleted
   553→	var filesToDelete []string
   554→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
   555→		if walkErr != nil {
   556→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
   557→		}
   558→
   559→		relPath, relErr := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
   560→		if relErr != nil {
   561→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
   562→		}
   563→
   564→		// Skip directories and protected paths
   565→		if info.IsDir() {
   566→			if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   567→				return filepath.SkipDir
   568→			}
   569→			return nil
   570→		}
   571→
   572→		// Skip files in protected directories
   573→		if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   574→			return nil
   575→		}
   576→
   577→		// If file is in checkpoint, it will be restored (not deleted)
   578→		if checkpointFiles[relPath] {
   579→			return nil
   580→		}
   581→
   582→		// If file is tracked in HEAD, don't delete (user's committed work)
   583→		if trackedFiles[relPath] {
   584→			return nil
   585→		}
   586→
   587→		// If file existed at session start, preserve it (untracked user files)
   588→		if preservedUntrackedFiles[relPath] {
   589→			return nil
   590→		}
   591→
   592→		// File is untracked and not in checkpoint - will be deleted
   593→		filesToDelete = append(filesToDelete, relPath)
   594→		return nil
   595→	})
   596→	if err != nil {
   597→		// Non-fatal, return what we have
   598→		return &RewindPreview{ //nolint:nilerr // Partial result is still useful
   599→			FilesToRestore: filesToRestore,
   600→			FilesToDelete:  filesToDelete,
   601→		}, nil
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	// Sort for consistent output
   605→	sort.Strings(filesToRestore)
   606→	sort.Strings(filesToDelete)
   607→
   608→	return &RewindPreview{
   609→		FilesToRestore: filesToRestore,
   610→		FilesToDelete:  filesToDelete,
   611→	}, nil
   612→}
   613→
   614→// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   615→// This fetches the transcript from entire/checkpoints/v1 and writes it to the agent's session directory.
   616→// Does not modify the working directory.
   617→// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.
   618→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   619→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error {
   620→	if !point.IsLogsOnly {
   621→		return errors.New("not a logs-only rewind point")
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	if point.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   625→		return errors.New("missing checkpoint ID")
   626→	}
   627→
   628→	// Resolve agent from checkpoint metadata (fall back to Claude for old checkpoints)
   629→	ag, err := ResolveAgentForRewind(point.Agent)
   630→	if err != nil {
   631→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve agent: %w", err)
   632→	}
   633→
   634→	// Get checkpoint store
   635→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   636→	if err != nil {
   637→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	// Read checkpoint summary to get session count
   641→	summary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)
   642→	if err != nil {
   643→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   644→	}
   645→	if summary == nil {
   646→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", point.CheckpointID)
   647→	}
   648→
   649→	// Get repo root for agent session directory lookup
   650→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   651→	if err != nil {
   652→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   653→	}
   654→
   655→	sessionDir, err := ag.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   656→	if err != nil {
   657→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent session directory: %w", err)
   658→	}
   659→
   660→	// Ensure session directory exists
   661→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   662→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   663→	}
   664→
   665→	// Check for newer local logs if not forcing
   666→	if !force {
   667→		sessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), sessionDir, ag, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)
   668→		hasConflicts := false
   669→		for _, sess := range sessions {
   670→			if sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   671→				hasConflicts = true
   672→				break
   673→			}
   674→		}
   675→		if hasConflicts {
   676→			shouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)
   677→			if promptErr != nil {
   678→				return promptErr
   679→			}
   680→			if !shouldOverwrite {
   681→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resume cancelled. Local session logs preserved.\n")
   682→				return nil
   683→			}
   684→		}
   685→	}
   686→
   687→	// Count sessions to restore
   688→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   689→	if totalSessions > 1 {
   690→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\n", totalSessions)
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	// Restore all sessions (oldest to newest, using 0-based indexing)
   694→	for i := range totalSessions {
   695→		content, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID, i)
   696→		if readErr != nil {
   697→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: failed to read session %d: %v\n", i, readErr)
   698→			continue
   699→		}
   700→		if content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   701→			continue
   702→		}
   703→
   704→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   705→		if sessionID == "" {
   706→			// Fallback: can't identify session without ID
   707→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: session %d has no session ID, skipping\n", i)
   708→			continue
   709→		}
   710→
   711→		sessionFile := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, ag, sessionDir)
   712→
   713→		// Get first prompt for display
   714→		promptPreview := ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts)
   715→
   716→		if totalSessions > 1 {
   717→			isLatest := i == totalSessions-1
   718→			if promptPreview != "" {
   719→				if isLatest {
   720→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d (latest): %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   721→				} else {
   722→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d: %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   723→				}
   724→			}
   725→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Writing to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   726→		} else {
   727→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing transcript to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   728→		}
   729→
   730→		// Ensure parent directory exists (session file may be in a different dir than sessionDir)
   731→		if mkdirErr := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); mkdirErr != nil {
   732→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Warning: failed to create directory: %v\n", mkdirErr)
   733→			continue
   734→		}
   735→
   736→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(sessionFile, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   737→			if totalSessions > 1 {
   738→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Warning: failed to write transcript: %v\n", writeErr)
   739→				continue
   740→			}
   741→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", writeErr)
   742→		}
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	return nil
   746→}
   747→
   748→// ResolveAgentForRewind resolves the agent from checkpoint metadata.
   749→// Falls back to the default agent (Claude) for old checkpoints that lack agent info.
   750→func ResolveAgentForRewind(agentType agent.AgentType) (agent.Agent, error) {
   751→	if !isSpecificAgentType(agentType) {
   752→		ag := agent.Default()
   753→		if ag == nil {
   754→			return nil, errors.New("no default agent registered")
   755→		}
   756→		return ag, nil
   757→	}
   758→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
   759→	if err != nil {
   760→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving agent %q: %w", agentType, err)
   761→	}
   762→	return ag, nil
   763→}
   764→
   765→// ResolveSessionFilePath determines the correct file path for an agent's session transcript.
   766→// Checks session state for transcript_path first (needed for agents like Gemini that store
   767→// transcripts at paths that GetSessionDir can't reconstruct, e.g. SHA-256 hashed directories).
   768→// Falls back to the agent's ExtractAgentSessionID + ResolveSessionFile with fallbackSessionDir.
   769→func ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID string, ag agent.Agent, fallbackSessionDir string) string {
   770→	state, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   771→	if err == nil && state != nil && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   772→		return state.TranscriptPath
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   776→	return ag.ResolveSessionFile(fallbackSessionDir, agentSessionID)
   777→}
   778→
   779→// readSessionPrompt reads the first prompt from the session's prompt.txt file stored in git.
   780→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   781→func readSessionPrompt(repo *git.Repository, commitHash plumbing.Hash, metadataDir string) string {
   782→	// Get the commit and its tree
   783→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   784→	if err != nil {
   785→		return ""
   786→	}
   787→
   788→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   789→	if err != nil {
   790→		return ""
   791→	}
   792→
   793→	// Look for prompt.txt in the metadata directory
   794→	promptPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.PromptFileName
   795→	promptEntry, err := tree.File(promptPath)
   796→	if err != nil {
   797→		return ""
   798→	}
   799→
   800→	content, err := promptEntry.Contents()
   801→	if err != nil {
   802→		return ""
   803→	}
   804→
   805→	return ExtractFirstPrompt(content)
   806→}
   807→
   808→// SessionRestoreStatus represents the status of a session being restored.
   809→type SessionRestoreStatus int
   810→
   811→const (
   812→	StatusNew             SessionRestoreStatus = iota // Local file doesn't exist
   813→	StatusUnchanged                                   // Local and checkpoint are the same
   814→	StatusCheckpointNewer                             // Checkpoint has newer entries
   815→	StatusLocalNewer                                  // Local has newer entries (conflict)
   816→)
   817→
   818→// SessionRestoreInfo contains information about a session being restored.
   819→type SessionRestoreInfo struct {
   820→	SessionID      string
   821→	Prompt         string               // First prompt preview for display
   822→	Status         SessionRestoreStatus // Status of this session
   823→	LocalTime      time.Time
   824→	CheckpointTime time.Time
   825→}
   826→
   827→// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info
   828→// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.
   829→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, sessionDir string, ag agent.Agent, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {
   830→	var sessions []SessionRestoreInfo
   831→
   832→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   833→	// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)
   834→	for i := range totalSessions {
   835→		content, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   836→		if err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   837→			continue
   838→		}
   839→
   840→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   841→		if sessionID == "" {
   842→			continue
   843→		}
   844→
   845→		localPath := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, ag, sessionDir)
   846→
   847→		localTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)
   848→		checkpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)
   849→		status := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)
   850→
   851→		sessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{
   852→			SessionID:      sessionID,
   853→			Prompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),
   854→			Status:         status,
   855→			LocalTime:      localTime,
   856→			CheckpointTime: checkpointTime,
   857→		})
   858→	}
   859→
   860→	return sessions
   861→}
   862→
   863→// ClassifyTimestamps determines the restore status based on local and checkpoint timestamps.
   864→func ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime time.Time) SessionRestoreStatus {
   865→	// Local file doesn't exist (no timestamp found)
   866→	if localTime.IsZero() {
   867→		return StatusNew
   868→	}
   869→
   870→	// Can't determine checkpoint time - treat as new/safe
   871→	if checkpointTime.IsZero() {
   872→		return StatusNew
   873→	}
   874→
   875→	// Compare timestamps
   876→	if localTime.After(checkpointTime) {
   877→		return StatusLocalNewer
   878→	}
   879→	if checkpointTime.After(localTime) {
   880→		return StatusCheckpointNewer
   881→	}
   882→	return StatusUnchanged
   883→}
   884→
   885→// StatusToText returns a human-readable status string.
   886→func StatusToText(status SessionRestoreStatus) string {
   887→	switch status {
   888→	case StatusNew:
   889→		return "(new)"
   890→	case StatusUnchanged:
   891→		return "(unchanged)"
   892→	case StatusCheckpointNewer:
   893→		return "(checkpoint is newer)"
   894→	case StatusLocalNewer:
   895→		return "(local is newer)" // shouldn't appear in non-conflict list
   896→	default:
   897→		return ""
   898→	}
   899→}
   900→
   901→// PromptOverwriteNewerLogs asks the user for confirmation to overwrite local
   902→// session logs that have newer timestamps than the checkpoint versions.
   903→func PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions []SessionRestoreInfo) (bool, error) {
   904→	// Separate conflicting and non-conflicting sessions
   905→	var conflicting, nonConflicting []SessionRestoreInfo
   906→	for _, s := range sessions {
   907→		if s.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   908→			conflicting = append(conflicting, s)
   909→		} else {
   910→			nonConflicting = append(nonConflicting, s)
   911→		}
   912→	}
   913→
   914→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: Local session log(s) have newer entries than the checkpoint:\n")
   915→	for _, info := range conflicting {
   916→		// Show prompt if available, otherwise fall back to session ID
   917→		if info.Prompt != "" {
   918→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  \"%s\"\n", info.Prompt)
   919→		} else {
   920→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session: %s\n", info.SessionID)
   921→		}
   922→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Local last entry:      %s\n", info.LocalTime.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   923→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Checkpoint last entry: %s\n", info.CheckpointTime.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   924→	}
   925→
   926→	// Show non-conflicting sessions with their status
   927→	if len(nonConflicting) > 0 {
   928→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nThese other session(s) will also be restored:\n")
   929→		for _, info := range nonConflicting {
   930→			statusText := StatusToText(info.Status)
   931→			if info.Prompt != "" {
   932→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  \"%s\" %s\n", info.Prompt, statusText)
   933→			} else {
   934→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session: %s %s\n", info.SessionID, statusText)
   935→			}
   936→		}
   937→	}
   938→
   939→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nOverwriting will lose the newer local entries.\n\n")
   940→
   941→	var confirmed bool
   942→	form := huh.NewForm(
   943→		huh.NewGroup(
   944→			huh.NewConfirm().
   945→				Title("Overwrite local session logs with checkpoint versions?").
   946→				Value(&confirmed),
   947→		),
   948→	)
   949→	if isAccessibleMode() {
   950→		form = form.WithAccessible(true)
   951→	}
   952→
   953→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   954→		if errors.Is(err, huh.ErrUserAborted) {
   955→			return false, nil
   956→		}
   957→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get confirmation: %w", err)
   958→	}
   959→
   960→	return confirmed, nil
   961→}
   962→

    32→// getAgent returns an agent by type
    33→func getAgent(agentType agentpkg.AgentType) (agentpkg.Agent, error) {
    34→	if agentType == "" {
    35→		return nil, errors.New("agent type cannot be empty")
    36→	}
    37→	ag, err := agentpkg.GetByAgentType(agentType)
    38→	if err != nil {
    39→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting agent: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→	return ag, nil
    42→}

    44→func newRewindCmd() *cobra.Command {
    45→	var listFlag bool
    46→	var toFlag string
    47→	var logsOnlyFlag bool
    48→	var resetFlag bool
    49→
    50→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:   "rewind",
    52→		Short: "Browse checkpoints and rewind your session",
    53→		Long: `Interactive command for rewinding and managing agent sessions.
    54→
    55→This command will show you an interactive list of recent checkpoints.  You'll be
    56→able to select one for Entire to rewind your branch state, including your code and
    57→your agent's context.`,
    58→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    59→			// Check if Entire is disabled
    60→			if checkDisabledGuard(cmd.OutOrStdout()) {
    61→				return nil
    62→			}
    63→
    64→			if listFlag {
    65→				return runRewindList()
    66→			}
    67→			if toFlag != "" {
    68→				return runRewindToWithOptions(toFlag, logsOnlyFlag, resetFlag)
    69→			}
    70→			return runRewindInteractive()
    71→		},
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&listFlag, "list", false, "List available rewind points (JSON output)")
    75→	cmd.Flags().StringVar(&toFlag, "to", "", "Rewind to specific commit ID (non-interactive)")
    76→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&logsOnlyFlag, "logs-only", false, "Only restore logs, don't modify working directory (for logs-only points)")
    77→	cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&resetFlag, "reset", false, "Reset branch to commit (destructive, for logs-only points)")
    78→
    79→	return cmd
    80→}

    82→func runRewindInteractive() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    83→	// Get the configured strategy
    84→	start := GetStrategy()
    85→
    86→	// Check for uncommitted changes first
    87→	canRewind, changeMsg, err := start.CanRewind()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check for uncommitted changes: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→	if !canRewind {
    92→		fmt.Println(changeMsg)
    93→		return nil
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Get rewind points from strategy
    97→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	if len(points) == 0 {
   103→		fmt.Println("No rewind points found.")
   104→		fmt.Println("Rewind points are created automatically when agent sessions end.")
   105→		return nil
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Check if there are multiple sessions (to show session identifier)
   109→	sessionIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   110→	for _, p := range points {
   111→		if p.SessionID != "" {
   112→			sessionIDs[p.SessionID] = true
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→	hasMultipleSessions := len(sessionIDs) > 1
   116→
   117→	// Build options for the select menu
   118→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(points)+1)
   119→	for _, p := range points {
   120→		var label string
   121→		timestamp := p.Date.Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
   122→
   123→		// Build session identifier for display when multiple sessions exist
   124→		sessionLabel := ""
   125→		if hasMultipleSessions && p.SessionPrompt != "" {
   126→			// Show truncated prompt to identify the session
   127→			sessionLabel = fmt.Sprintf(" [%s]", sanitizeForTerminal(p.SessionPrompt))
   128→		}
   129→
   130→		switch {
   131→		case p.IsLogsOnly:
   132→			// Committed checkpoint - show commit sha (this is the real user commit)
   133→			shortID := p.ID
   134→			if len(shortID) >= 7 {
   135→				shortID = shortID[:7]
   136→			}
   137→			label = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s) %s%s", shortID, timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   138→		case p.IsTaskCheckpoint:
   139→			// Task checkpoint (uncommitted) - no sha shown
   140→			label = fmt.Sprintf("        (%s) [Task] %s%s", timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   141→		default:
   142→			// Shadow checkpoint (uncommitted) - no sha shown (internal commit)
   143→			label = fmt.Sprintf("        (%s) %s%s", timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   144→		}
   145→		options = append(options, huh.NewOption(label, p.ID))
   146→	}
   147→	options = append(options, huh.NewOption("Cancel", "cancel"))
   148→
   149→	var selectedID string
   150→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   151→		huh.NewGroup(
   152→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   153→				Title("Select a checkpoint to restore").
   154→				Description("Your working directory will be restored to this checkpoint's state").
   155→				Options(options...).
   156→				Value(&selectedID),
   157→		),
   158→	)
   159→
   160→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   161→		return fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	if selectedID == "cancel" {
   165→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   166→		return nil
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Find the selected point
   170→	var selectedPoint *strategy.RewindPoint
   171→	for _, p := range points {
   172→		if p.ID == selectedID {
   173→			pointCopy := p
   174→			selectedPoint = &pointCopy
   175→			break
   176→		}
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	if selectedPoint == nil {
   180→		return errors.New("rewind point not found")
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	shortID := selectedPoint.ID
   184→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
   185→		shortID = shortID[:7]
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Show what was selected
   189→	switch {
   190→	case selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly:
   191→		// Committed checkpoint - show sha
   192→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: %s %s\n", shortID, sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   193→	case selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint:
   194→		// Task checkpoint - no sha
   195→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: [Task] %s\n", sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   196→	default:
   197→		// Shadow checkpoint - no sha
   198→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: %s\n", sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Handle logs-only points with a sub-choice menu
   202→	if selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly {
   203→		return handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive(start, *selectedPoint, shortID)
   204→	}
   205→
   206→	// Preview rewind to show warnings about files that will be deleted
   207→	preview, previewErr := start.PreviewRewind(*selectedPoint)
   208→	if previewErr == nil && preview != nil && len(preview.FilesToDelete) > 0 {
   209→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: The following untracked files will be DELETED:\n")
   210→		for _, f := range preview.FilesToDelete {
   211→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", f)
   212→		}
   213→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\n")
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Confirm rewind
   217→	var confirm bool
   218→	description := fmt.Sprintf("This will reset to: %s\nChanges after this point may be lost!", selectedPoint.Message)
   219→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
   220→		huh.NewGroup(
   221→			huh.NewConfirm().
   222→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Reset to %s?", shortID)).
   223→				Description(description).
   224→				Value(&confirm),
   225→		),
   226→	)
   227→
   228→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
   229→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	if !confirm {
   233→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   234→		return nil
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   238→	agent, err := getAgent(selectedPoint.Agent)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   244→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   245→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   246→
   247→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind started",
   248→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   249→		slog.String("session_id", selectedPoint.SessionID),
   250→		slog.Bool("is_task_checkpoint", selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint),
   251→	)
   252→
   253→	// Perform the rewind using strategy
   254→	if err := start.Rewind(*selectedPoint); err != nil {
   255→		logging.Error(ctx, "rewind failed",
   256→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   257→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   258→		)
   259→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind completed",
   263→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   264→	)
   265→
   266→	// Handle transcript restoration differently for task checkpoints
   267→	var sessionID string
   268→	var transcriptFile string
   269→
   270→	if selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   271→		// For task checkpoint: read checkpoint.json to get UUID and truncate transcript
   272→		checkpoint, err := start.GetTaskCheckpoint(*selectedPoint)
   273→		if err != nil {
   274→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read task checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   275→			return nil
   276→		}
   277→
   278→		sessionID = checkpoint.SessionID
   279→
   280→		if checkpoint.CheckpointUUID != "" {
   281→			// Truncate transcript at checkpoint UUID
   282→			if err := restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(start, *selectedPoint, sessionID, checkpoint.CheckpointUUID, agent); err != nil {
   283→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore truncated session transcript: %v\n", err)
   284→			} else {
   285→				fmt.Printf("Rewound to task checkpoint. %s\n", formatResumeCommand(sessionID, agent))
   286→			}
   287→			return nil
   288→		}
   289→	} else {
   290→		// For session checkpoint: restore full transcript
   291→		// Prefer SessionID from trailer (set by GetRewindPoints from Entire-Session trailer)
   292→		// over path-based extraction which is less reliable.
   293→		sessionID = selectedPoint.SessionID
   294→		if sessionID == "" {
   295→			sessionID = extractSessionIDFromMetadata(selectedPoint.MetadataDir)
   296→		}
   297→		transcriptFile = filepath.Join(selectedPoint.MetadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy)
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Try to restore transcript using the appropriate method:
   301→	// 1. Checkpoint storage (committed checkpoints with valid checkpoint ID)
   302→	// 2. Shadow branch (uncommitted checkpoints with commit hash)
   303→	// 3. Local file (active sessions)
   304→	var restored bool
   305→	if !selectedPoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   306→		// Try checkpoint storage first for committed checkpoints
   307→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(selectedPoint.CheckpointID, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   308→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   309→			restored = true
   310→		}
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if !restored && selectedPoint.MetadataDir != "" && len(selectedPoint.ID) == 40 {
   314→		// Try shadow branch for uncommitted checkpoints (ID is a 40-char commit hash)
   315→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(selectedPoint.ID, selectedPoint.MetadataDir, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   316→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   317→			restored = true
   318→		}
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	if !restored {
   322→		// Fall back to local file
   323→		if err := restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID, agent); err != nil {
   324→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore session transcript: %v\n", err)
   325→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Source: %s\n", transcriptFile)
   326→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	fmt.Printf("Rewound to %s. %s\n", shortID, formatResumeCommand(sessionID, agent))
   331→	return nil
   332→}

   334→func runRewindList() error {
   335→	start := GetStrategy()
   336→
   337→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
   338→	if err != nil {
   339→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Output as JSON for programmatic use
   343→	type jsonPoint struct {
   344→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   345→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   346→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   347→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   348→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   349→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
   350→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   351→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id,omitempty"`
   352→		SessionID        string `json:"session_id,omitempty"`
   353→		SessionPrompt    string `json:"session_prompt,omitempty"`
   354→	}
   355→
   356→	output := make([]jsonPoint, len(points))
   357→	for i, p := range points {
   358→		output[i] = jsonPoint{
   359→			ID:               p.ID,
   360→			Message:          p.Message,
   361→			MetadataDir:      p.MetadataDir,
   362→			Date:             p.Date.Format(time.RFC3339),
   363→			IsTaskCheckpoint: p.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   364→			ToolUseID:        p.ToolUseID,
   365→			IsLogsOnly:       p.IsLogsOnly,
   366→			CondensationID:   p.CheckpointID.String(),
   367→			SessionID:        p.SessionID,
   368→			SessionPrompt:    p.SessionPrompt,
   369→		}
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	// Print as JSON
   373→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(output, "", "  ")
   374→	if err != nil {
   375→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   376→	}
   377→	fmt.Println(string(data))
   378→	return nil
   379→}
