> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Summary of changes

### `strategy/strategy.go`
- Added `RestoredSession` struct with `SessionID`, `Agent`, and `Prompt` fields
- Changed `LogsOnlyRestorer.RestoreLogsOnly` return type from `error` to `([]RestoredSession, error)`

### `strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`
- **`RestoreLogsOnly()`**: Now resolves a per-session agent from `content.Metadata.Agent` (falling back to the outer agent for old checkpoints without agent info). Returns `[]RestoredSession` so callers can print correct per-session resume commands
- **`classifySessionsForRestore()`**: Changed `sessionDir` parameter to `repoRoot` and now resolves per-session agents for correct local path classification
- Removed the single outer `sessionDir` setup — each session computes its own agent dir

### `rewind.go`
- **`getAgent()`**: Now delegates to `strategy.ResolveAgentForRewind()` (wrapping the error), fixing the inconsistency where `getAgent("")` errored but `ResolveAgentForRewind("")` fell back to Claude
- **`printMultiSessionResumeCommands()`**: Changed signature from `(point, agent)` to `(sessions []strategy.RestoredSession)`, resolving per-session agents from each `RestoredSession`
- Updated all 5 `RestoreLogsOnly` call sites to capture `[]RestoredSession` and pass to `printMultiSessionResumeCommands`

### `resume.go`
- Simplified the `resumeSession` caller: removed ~40 lines of re-read logic (re-opening repo, re-reading metadata tree, re-reading prompts). Now uses `[]RestoredSession` directly from `RestoreLogsOnly`
- Added `Session:` line for single-session output to maintain backward compatibility with existing output format

   639→	}

   642→	summary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)
   643→	if err != nil {
   644→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   645→	}
   646→	if summary == nil {

   655→

   658→		sessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), repoRoot, outerAgent, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)
   659→		hasConflicts := false
   660→		for _, sess := range sessions {
   661→			if sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   662→				hasConflicts = true
   663→				break
   664→			}
   665→		}
   666→		if hasConflicts {
   667→			shouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)
   668→			if promptErr != nil {
   669→				return nil, promptErr
   670→			}
   671→			if !shouldOverwrite {

   673→				return nil, nil
   674→			}
   675→		}
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Count sessions to restore

   680→	if totalSessions > 1 {
   681→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\n", totalSessions)
   682→	}

1. Remove the outer `outerAgent` fallback in `RestoreLogsOnly`
2. In the per-session loop, if a session has no agent metadata, warn and skip it
3. Same in `classifySessionsForRestore` — remove `outerAgent` parameter, skip sessions without agent
4. `ResolveAgentForRewind` still needs to exist for callers in `rewind.go` etc., but `RestoreLogsOnly` should not use the fallback

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info\n// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.\n// repoRoot is used to compute per-session agent directories.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, outerAgent agent.Agent, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {\n\tvar sessions []SessionRestoreInfo\n\n\ttotalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)\n\t// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)\n\tfor i := range totalSessions {\n\t\tcontent, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)\n\t\tif err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID\n\t\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Resolve per-session agent (fall back to outer agent for old checkpoints)\n\t\tsessionAgent := outerAgent\n\t\tif content.Metadata.Agent != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif perAg, agErr := ResolveAgentForRewind(content.Metadata.Agent); agErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\tsessionAgent = perAg\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionAgentDir, dirErr := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)\n\t\tif dirErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tlocalPath := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)\n\n\t\tlocalTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)\n\t\tcheckpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)\n\t\tstatus := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)\n\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{\n\t\t\tSessionID:      sessionID,\n\t\t\tPrompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),\n\t\t\tStatus:         status,\n\t\t\tLocalTime:      localTime,\n\t\t\tCheckpointTime: checkpointTime,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn sessions\n}",
  "new_string": "// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info\n// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.\n// repoRoot is used to compute per-session agent directories.\n// Sessions without agent metadata are skipped (cannot determine target directory).\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {\n\tvar sessions []SessionRestoreInfo\n\n\ttotalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)\n\t// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)\n\tfor i := range totalSessions {\n\t\tcontent, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)\n\t\tif err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID\n\t\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Resolve per-session agent — skip sessions without agent metadata\n\t\tif content.Metadata.Agent == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tsessionAgent, agErr := ResolveAgentForRewind(content.Metadata.Agent)\n\t\tif agErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionAgentDir, dirErr := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)\n\t\tif dirErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tlocalPath := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)\n\n\t\tlocalTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)\n\t\tcheckpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)\n\t\tstatus := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)\n\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{\n\t\t\tSessionID:      sessionID,\n\t\t\tPrompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),\n\t\t\tStatus:         status,\n\t\t\tLocalTime:      localTime,\n\t\t\tCheckpointTime: checkpointTime,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn sessions\n}"
}

> AGENT

All green. Here's what changed:

- **`RestoreLogsOnly`**: Removed the outer `outerAgent` fallback. Each session must have `content.Metadata.Agent` set — if it's missing, a warning is printed and the session is skipped: `Warning: session N (session-id) has no agent metadata, skipping (cannot determine target directory)`. Same for unknown agent types.
- **`classifySessionsForRestore`**: Removed the `outerAgent` parameter. Sessions without agent metadata are silently skipped during classification.
- **`resume.go`**: When `RestoreLogsOnly` returns an empty slice (all sessions skipped due to missing agent metadata), falls back to `resumeSingleSession` which uses the checkpoint-level agent. This preserves backward compatibility for old checkpoints.

No `logging` import. Per the CLAUDE.md guidelines, operational messages should use the `logging` package. But this is a classification helper (not a command entry point), and the `RestoreLogsOnly` method already prints stderr warnings for the same conditions. Adding `logging` import here just for these skip messages would be heavier than needed. I'll use `fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, ...)` to match the existing pattern in this file (e.g., lines 689, 699, 713).

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed to trace the full path resolution chain. Let me compile a comprehensive response.

## File Path Resolution Chain for Gemini CLI Session Transcripts

I've traced the complete path resolution chain from session ID to final file path on disk. Here's the full picture:

### 1. **Entry Points & Session ID**

When a Gemini hook is invoked, the `transcript_path` is provided directly by Gemini in the hook JSON:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/types.go` (lines 46-55)
```go
type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`  // Direct path from Gemini hook
    // ...
}
```

The transcript path is extracted during hook parsing in `gemini.go` (lines 108-109):
```go
input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath  // Stored as SessionRef
```

### 2. **Session State Storage**

The transcript path is persisted in session state at `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json`:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session/state.go` (lines 108-109)
```go
// TranscriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection)
TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path,omitempty"`
```

It's initialized in the strategy when the session starts:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_session.go` (lines 189-241)
```go
// initializeSession creates a new session state...
// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) initializeSession(repo *git.Repository, sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) (*SessionState, error) {
    // ...
    state := &SessionState{
        SessionID:      sessionID,
        // ...
        TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,  // Stored here
        FirstPrompt:    truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
    }
    // ...
}
```

### 3. **Path Resolution During Rewind**

When restoring logs from a checkpoint, the `ResolveSessionFilePath()` function determines the final file path:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` (lines 773-785)

```go
// ResolveSessionFilePath determines the correct file path for an agent's session transcript.
// Checks session state for transcript_path first (needed for agents like Gemini that store
// transcripts at paths that GetSessionDir can't reconstruct, e.g. SHA-256 hashed directories).
// Falls back to the agent's ExtractAgentSessionID + ResolveSessionFile with fallbackSessionDir.
func ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID string, ag agent.Agent, fallbackSessionDir string) string {
	state, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
	if err == nil && state != nil && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
		return state.TranscriptPath  // PRIMARY: Use stored path directly
	}

	agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
	return ag.ResolveSessionFile(fallbackSessionDir, agentSessionID)  // FALLBACK
}
```

### 4. **Session ID Transformation (For Backward Compatibility)**

The `ExtractAgentSessionID()` function strips legacy date prefixes:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` (lines 166-171)
```go
// ExtractAgentSessionID extracts the Gemini session ID from an Entire session ID.
// Since Entire session ID = agent session ID (identity), this returns the input unchanged.
// For backwards compatibility with legacy date-prefixed IDs, it strips the prefix if present.
func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractAgentSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {
	return sessionid.ModelSessionID(entireSessionID)
}
```

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/sessionid/sessionid.go` (lines 13-24)
```go
// ModelSessionID extracts the agent session UUID from an Entire session ID.
// For backwards compatibility with old date-prefixed session IDs (YYYY-MM-DD-<uuid>),
// it strips the date prefix if present.
func ModelSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {
	// Check for legacy format: YYYY-MM-DD-<agent-uuid> (11 chars prefix: "2026-01-23-")
	if len(entireSessionID) > 11 && entireSessionID[4] == '-' && entireSessionID[7] == '-' && entireSessionID[10] == '-' {
		return entireSessionID[11:]
	}
	// Return as-is (new format: agent session ID = entire session ID)
	return entireSessionID
}
```

### 5. **Fallback Path Resolution (If No TranscriptPath in Session State)**

If the session state doesn't have a `TranscriptPath`, `ResolveSessionFile()` is called:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` (lines 176-197)
```go
// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
	shortID := agentSessionID
	if len(shortID) > 8 {
		shortID = shortID[:8]  // Use first 8 chars only
	}

	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
		return matches[len(matches)-1]
	}

	// Fallback: construct a default path
	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
}
```

The `sessionDir` itself is computed by `GetSessionDir()`:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` (lines 199-215)
```go
// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
	// Check for test environment override
	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
		return override, nil
	}

	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
	}

	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
}
```

The path sanitization function:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` (lines 280-286)
```go
// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)

func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
}
```

### 6. **Actual Path Usage During Rewind**

The resolved path is used to write the transcript back to disk:

**File:** `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` (lines 620-754)
```go
// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error) {
	// ... checkpoint reading ...
	
	sessionFile := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)

	// ... permission checks ...

	// Ensure parent directory exists
	if mkdirErr := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); mkdirErr != nil {
		// handle error
	}

	if writeErr := os.WriteFile(sessionFile, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
		// handle error
	}

	restored = append(restored, RestoredSession{
		SessionID: sessionID,
		Agent:     sessionAgent.Type(),
		Prompt:    promptPreview,
	})
}
```

### Directory Structure Summary

**Gemini CLI stores transcripts at:**
```
~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized-repo-path>/chats/session-<date>-<shortid>.json
```

Example for repo at `/Users/me/projects/myapp`:
```
~/.gemini/tmp/Users-me-projects-myapp/chats/session-2026-01-15-a1b2c3d4.json
```

**Session state is stored at:**
```
<repo>/.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
```

Contains:
- `transcript_path`: The live transcript path (populated at session start from Gemini hook)
- `session_id`: The Entire session ID (same as agent session ID, with legacy date prefix stripped)
- `agent_type`: "Gemini CLI"
- Other session metadata

### Full Path Resolution Chain

```
1. Gemini Hook fires
   ↓
2. transcript_path extracted from JSON
   ↓
3. Stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json as TranscriptPath field
   ↓
4. During rewind, LoadSessionState(sessionID) retrieves it
   ↓
5. ResolveSessionFilePath() checks if TranscriptPath exists in state
   ↓
6. IF YES → Return state.TranscriptPath directly
   IF NO  → Fall back to GetSessionDir() + ResolveSessionFile()
              (searches for session-*-<shortid>.json pattern)
   ↓
7. Write restored transcript to final path via os.WriteFile()
```

This two-tier approach is crucial for Gemini because its project directories are SHA-256 hashes that can't be reconstructed from just the repo path—the hook provides the exact path that Gemini computed internally, and we preserve it for perfect resumption.

Here's the path resolution chain for Gemini transcripts during restore:

### Resolution order (`ResolveSessionFilePath`)

1. **Primary — session state**: Check `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json` for a stored `transcript_path`. If present, use it directly. This is the path Gemini provided in its hook JSON at session start.

2. **Fallback — glob match**: `GetSessionDir()` computes `~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized-repo-path>/chats/`, then `ResolveSessionFile()` globs for `session-*-<first-8-chars-of-id>.json`. Returns the last (most recent) match.

3. **Last resort**: `<sessionDir>/<agentSessionID>.json`

### Concrete example

For a repo at `/Users/me/myapp` with session ID `abc12345-6789-...`:

```
# Primary (from stored transcript_path):
~/.gemini/tmp/Users-me-myapp/chats/session-2026-01-15-abc12345.json

# Fallback (glob):
~/.gemini/tmp/Users-me-myapp/chats/session-*-abc12345.json → picks last match

# Last resort:
~/.gemini/tmp/Users-me-myapp/chats/abc12345-6789-....json
```

The key insight: Gemini's actual transcript path comes from the hook at session start and is stored in session state. The fallback only kicks in when session state is missing (e.g., restoring on a different machine). Is there a specific concern about this path resolution?

     1→# Fix: Per-session agent resolution in multi-session checkpoints
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→A checkpoint can contain multiple sessions from different agents (e.g., session 0 from Claude, session 1 from Gemini). The per-session agent type **is stored correctly** in each session's `metadata.json` on `entire/checkpoints/v1` (`CommittedMetadata.Agent`), but the consumption layer collapses everything to a single agent — always session 0's. This means session 1's transcript gets written to the wrong directory, with the wrong file extension, and the wrong resume command.

Rather than threading `SessionAgents` arrays through `CheckpointInfo` → `RewindPoint` → consumers, we resolve per-session agents **at consumption time** inside `RestoreLogsOnly()` and return the per-session info so callers can print correct resume commands.

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive summary of my findings:

## Summary of Checkpoint Metadata Storage

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here's what metadata is available in committed checkpoints for Gemini sessions:

### 1. **CommittedMetadata struct** (stored in `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch)
Location: `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/checkpoint.go:330-362`

**Available Fields:**
- `CLIVersion` - Version of the CLI that created the checkpoint
- `CheckpointID` - 12-hex-char stable identifier (e.g., `a3b2c4d5e6f7`)
- `SessionID` - Session identifier
- `Strategy` - Strategy name (`manual-commit` or `auto-commit`)
- **`CreatedAt`** - **Timestamp when checkpoint was created** ✓ AVAILABLE
- `Branch` - Branch where checkpoint was created (empty if detached HEAD)
- `CheckpointsCount` - Number of checkpoints in session
- `FilesTouched` - List of files modified during session
- **`Agent`** - Agent type (e.g., "Gemini", "Claude Code") ✓ AVAILABLE
- `IsTask` / `ToolUseID` - Task checkpoint fields
- `TranscriptIdentifierAtStart` - Last identifier when checkpoint started (message ID for Gemini)
- `CheckpointTranscriptStart` - Transcript line offset at checkpoint start
- `TokenUsage` - Token usage information
- `Summary` - AI-generated summary
- `InitialAttribution` - Line-level attribution metrics

### 2. **SessionContent struct** - What RestoreLogsOnly reads
Location: `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/checkpoint/checkpoint.go:312-327`

When `RestoreLogsOnly` is called (line 614-754 in `manual_commit_rewind.go`):
1. It reads the `CheckpointSummary` from root metadata
2. For each session index, it calls `ReadSessionContent()` which returns:
   - `Metadata` (CommittedMetadata) - **CONTAINS ALL FIELDS ABOVE**
   - `Transcript` - Full session transcript bytes
   - `Prompts` - User prompts text
   - `Context` - Generated context.md content

### 3. **Key Finding: projectHash is NOT stored**
Grep search: `projectHash|project_hash` returned **no results** in the entire codebase.

**There is NO projectHash field in:**
- CommittedMetadata struct
- SessionContent struct
- SaveContext struct (what flows from hooks to storage)
- CheckpointSummary struct
- Any hook input structures from Gemini CLI

### 4. **What IS available in RestoreLogsOnly:**
- ✓ **SessionID** - `content.Metadata.SessionID`
- ✓ **CreatedAt** - `content.Metadata.CreatedAt` (session start time in UTC)
- ✓ **Agent** - `content.Metadata.Agent` (e.g., "Gemini")
- ✓ **SessionPrompts** - `content.Prompts` (extracted user prompts)
- ✓ **Transcript** - `content.Transcript` (full conversation history)
- ✓ **Token Usage** - `content.Metadata.TokenUsage` (API usage stats)

### 5. **How metadata flows from Gemini hooks to checkpoint storage:**

The SaveContext (line 256-271 in `hooks_geminicli_handlers.go`) is built with:
- SessionID (from hook input)
- ModifiedFiles, NewFiles, DeletedFiles (from transcript analysis)
- MetadataDir, CommitMessage
- AuthorName, AuthorEmail
- **AgentType** - Set to `hookAgent.Type()` (will be "Gemini")
- **StepTranscriptIdentifier** & **StepTranscriptStart** - From pre-prompt state
- **TokenUsage** - Calculated from transcript

This SaveContext is passed to `strategy.SaveChanges()` which writes it to `WriteCommittedOptions` and eventually gets stored in `CommittedMetadata.CreatedAt` (set at write time via `time.Now().UTC()` in line 337 of `committed.go`).

### Conclusion:
**projectHash is NOT available in committed checkpoint metadata.** The only time-related field available is `CreatedAt` (session start time), and the agent information is available via the `Agent` field. If you need a project hash, it would need to be:
1. Explicitly captured at session start and stored in the metadata
2. Or computed from the repository state at `CreatedAt` time
3. Or stored as part of the `RawData` field in HookInput if agent-specific

Excellent! Now I have enough information. Let me create a comprehensive summary document. Based on my analysis, here's the comprehensive exploration of Gemini session path resolution and checkpoint data:

## Summary: Gemini Session Path Resolution & Checkpoint Data Reconstruction

### 1. **Gemini Session Path Resolution Flow**

The Gemini path resolution happens in this sequence:

#### Path Structure
```
~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized-repo-path>/chats/session-<date>-<shortid>.json
```

**Key Components:**
- **Sanitized repo path** (`SanitizePathForGemini`): Replaces all non-alphanumeric characters with `-`
  - Example: `/Users/user/my-repo` → `Users-user-my-repo`
- **Filename format**: `session-*-<first8chars-of-sessionid>.json`
  - Example: `session-2026-02-10T09-19-0544a0f5.json` (if sessionID is `44a0f5...`)

#### Resolution Priority in `GetSessionDir()` (gemini.go, line 199-214)
```go
func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
    homeDir, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
    projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)  // Replace non-alphanumeric with -
    return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
}
```

#### Session File Lookup in `ResolveSessionFile()` (gemini.go, line 176-197)
1. **Pattern matching**: Globs for `session-*-<first8chars>.json`
2. **Fallback**: Returns `<sessiondir>/<sessionid>.json` if no match found
3. **Selection**: Returns the most recent match (last alphabetically, since dates are sortable)

### 2. **Available Data at Restore Time**

When `RestoreLogsOnly()` is called in `manual_commit_rewind.go` (line 620-753), the following data is available for reconstruction:

#### From `RewindPoint` Parameter
- `ID`: Commit hash (for logs-only points)
- `CheckpointID`: 12-hex-char stable ID (e.g., `a3b2c4d5e6f7`)
- `IsLogsOnly`: Boolean indicating it's a logs-only point
- Not typically `AgentSessionID` here

#### From `SessionContent` (via `store.ReadSessionContent()`, line 681)
```go
type SessionContent struct {
    Metadata   CommittedMetadata  // Session-specific metadata
    Transcript []byte             // Raw JSONL transcript
    Prompts    string             // User prompts from session
    Context    string             // context.md content
}
```

**Critical fields in `CommittedMetadata`:**
```go
type CommittedMetadata struct {
    SessionID   string         // e.g., "2026-02-10-session-id-uuid"
    Agent       agent.AgentType // e.g., "gemini-cli"
    Strategy    string         // "manual-commit" or "auto-commit"
    CreatedAt   time.Time      // Checkpoint creation timestamp
    // ... other fields ...
}
```

#### From Session State (via `LoadSessionState()`)
```go
type State struct {  // Alias for session.State
    SessionID           string
    TranscriptPath      string     // ★ CRITICAL: Path to live transcript file!
    AgentType           agent.AgentType
    BaseCommit          string
    WorktreePath        string
    // ... other fields ...
}
```

### 3. **Critical Data: `TranscriptPath` Field**

**This is the key to Gemini path reconstruction!**

**Where it's stored:**
- In `.git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json` (line 109 in `session/state.go`)
- Populated when session starts (via hook input parsing)
- Persists for lifetime of session

**What it contains:**
- **Absolute path** to the Gemini session transcript file
- Example: `/Users/user/.gemini/tmp/my-repo-name/chats/session-2026-02-10T09-19-0544a0f5.json`
- Captured from Gemini's hook input in `ParseHookInput()` (gemini.go line 108-109)

**In RestoreLogsOnly() code flow (line 713):**
```go
sessionFile := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)
```

This function (line 773-785 in `manual_commit_rewind.go`) checks:
1. Load session state by `sessionID`
2. If `state.TranscriptPath` exists, **return it directly** ✓
3. Otherwise compute fallback path using `GetSessionDir()` + `ResolveSessionFile()`

### 4. **Data Available for Path Reconstruction**

**At logs-only restore time:**

| Data Source | Field | Available | Purpose |
|-------------|-------|-----------|---------|
| **RewindPoint** | CheckpointID | ✓ | Links to checkpoint on metadata branch |
| **RewindPoint** | SessionID | ✓ | Session identifier |
| **SessionContent.Metadata** | Agent | ✓ | Identifies "gemini-cli" |
| **SessionContent.Metadata** | SessionID | ✓ | Matches RewindPoint.SessionID |
| **Session State** | TranscriptPath | ✓ | **Direct path to file** |
| **Session State** | AgentType | ✓ | "gemini-cli" for validation |
| **Checkpoint repo path** | (inferred) | ✓ | Available from `paths.RepoRoot()` |

### 5. **Metadata Stored in Checkpoint**

**What's available in CheckpointSummary (root metadata.json):**
- `CheckpointID`: 12-hex checkpoint ID
- `SessionCount`: Number of sessions (1+ for multi-session checkpoints)
- `Sessions[]`: Array of session file paths (not session-specific data)
- `Strategy`: "manual-commit" or "auto-commit"

**What's available in CommittedMetadata (session-level):**
- `SessionID`: The session identifier
- `Agent`: agent.AgentType indicating which agent (e.g., "gemini-cli")
- `CreatedAt`: When checkpoint was created
- `CheckpointsCount`: Checkpoints in this session
- `FilesTouched`: List of modified files
- `TranscriptIdentifierAtStart`: Message ID for Gemini (for chunked transcript resumption)

### 6. **Critical Insight: Multi-Session Checkpoints**

From `committed.go` (line 250-277):

**Session storage structure** on `entire/checkpoints/v1` branch:
```
<checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/
├── metadata.json          # CheckpointSummary (root level)
├── 0/                     # Session 0 (numbered subdirectory)
│   ├── metadata.json      # CommittedMetadata
│   ├── full.jsonl
│   ├── prompt.txt
│   └── context.md
├── 1/                     # Session 1 (if multiple sessions)
└── ...
```

**Multiple sessions scenario (line 673-753):**
- When restoring from a multi-session checkpoint, iterate through `sessionIndex 0..N-1`
- Each session has its own `CommittedMetadata` with `SessionID`
- Can read each session independently via `ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, sessionIndex)`

### 7. **Path Reconstruction Without TranscriptPath**

**Fallback logic** (line 777-784 in `manual_commit_rewind.go`):
```go
func ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID string, ag agent.Agent, fallbackSessionDir string) string {
    state, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
    if err == nil && state != nil && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
        return state.TranscriptPath  // ✓ DIRECT PATH
    }
    
    agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
    return ag.ResolveSessionFile(fallbackSessionDir, agentSessionID)
}
```

**Fallback computation:**
1. Call `GetSessionDir(repoPath)` → `~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized>/chats`
2. Call `ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID)`:
   - Extract first 8 chars of `agentSessionID`
   - Glob for `session-*-<first8chars>.json`
   - Return most recent match or default to `<sessionid>.json`

### 8. **Key Functions Summary**

| Function | Location | Purpose |
|----------|----------|---------|
| `SanitizePathForGemini()` | gemini.go:284 | Convert path to directory name |
| `GetSessionDir()` | gemini.go:201 | Return `~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized>/chats` |
| `ResolveSessionFile()` | gemini.go:180 | Find/construct session filename |
| `LoadSessionState()` | session_state.go:41 | Load `.git/entire-sessions/<id>.json` |
| `ResolveSessionFilePath()` | manual_commit_rewind.go:773 | Main resolution entry point |
| `ReadSessionContent()` | checkpoint/committed.go:688 | Read session metadata from checkpoint |

### 9. **Answer to Core Questions**

**Q: What data do we have available at restore time?**

✓ **SessionID** - primary identifier
✓ **Agent type** - tells us it's Gemini
✓ **TranscriptPath** - cached from session state (if session still active)
✓ **Repository path** - available via `paths.RepoRoot()`
✓ **Session-level metadata** - from `CommittedMetadata` in checkpoint
✓ **Multi-session info** - from `CheckpointSummary.Sessions` array

**Q: Can we reconstruct Gemini path from checkpoint content alone?**

✓ **YES** via fallback logic:
1. Sanitize repo path → `Users-gtrrz-victor-wks-cli` (example)
2. Construct base dir → `~/.gemini/tmp/Users-gtrrz-victor-wks-cli/chats`
3. Use glob pattern → `session-*-<first8chars>.json`
4. Select most recent match

**However**, if `TranscriptPath` exists in session state, it's **the authoritative source** and should be used directly (faster, more reliable).

    51→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    52→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    53→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    54→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    55→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    58→		repoRoot = "."
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Check for .gemini directory
    62→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    63→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    64→		return true, nil
    65→	}
    66→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    67→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    68→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    69→		return true, nil
    70→	}
    71→	return false, nil
    72→}
    73→
    74→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    75→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    76→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    77→}

    79→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    80→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    81→	return true
    82→}
    83→
    84→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    85→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    86→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    87→	if err != nil {
    88→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	if len(data) == 0 {
    92→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    96→		HookType:  hookType,
    97→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    98→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	// Parse based on hook type
   102→	switch hookType {
   103→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   104→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   105→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   106→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   107→		}
   108→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   109→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   110→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   111→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   112→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   113→		if raw.Source != "" {
   114→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   115→		}
   116→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   117→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   118→		}
   119→
   120→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   121→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   122→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   123→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   124→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   125→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   126→		}
   127→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   128→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   129→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   130→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   131→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   132→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   133→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   134→		}
   135→
   136→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   137→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   138→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   139→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   140→		}
   141→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   142→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   143→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   144→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   145→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   146→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   147→		}
   148→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   149→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	return input, nil
   153→}
   154→

   156→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   157→	return input.SessionID

   162→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) TransformSessionID(agentSessionID string) string {
   163→	return agentSessionID
   164→}

   166→// ExtractAgentSessionID extracts the Gemini session ID from an Entire session ID.
   167→// Since Entire session ID = agent session ID (identity), this returns the input unchanged.
   168→// For backwards compatibility with legacy date-prefixed IDs, it strips the prefix if present.
   169→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractAgentSessionID(entireSessionID string) string {
   170→	return sessionid.ModelSessionID(entireSessionID)

   172→
   173→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
   174→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
   175→
   176→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
   177→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
   178→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
   179→// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
   180→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   181→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
   182→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
   183→	shortID := agentSessionID
   184→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   185→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
   189→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
   190→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
   191→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
   192→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Fallback: construct a default path
   196→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   197→}
   198→
   199→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   200→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   201→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   202→	// Check for test environment override
   203→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   204→		return override, nil
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   208→	if err != nil {
   209→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
   213→	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
   214→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   215→}
   216→
   217→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   218→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   219→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   220→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   221→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Read the raw JSON file
   225→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   228→	}
   229→
   230→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   231→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   232→	if err != nil {
   233→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   234→		modifiedFiles = nil
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   238→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   239→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   240→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   241→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   242→		NativeData:    data,
   243→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   244→	}, nil
   245→}
   246→
   247→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   248→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   249→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   250→	if session == nil {
   251→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   255→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   256→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   260→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   261→	}
   262→
   263→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   264→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Write the raw JSON data
   268→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   269→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	return nil
   273→}
   274→
   275→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   276→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   277→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   278→}
   279→
   280→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   281→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   282→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   283→
   284→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   285→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   286→}
   287→
   288→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   289→
   290→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   291→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   292→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   293→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   294→	if path == "" {
   295→		return 0, nil
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   299→	if err != nil {
   300→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   301→			return 0, nil
   302→		}
   303→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	if len(data) == 0 {
   307→		return 0, nil
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   311→	if err != nil {
   312→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   316→}
   317→
   318→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   319→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   320→// Returns:
   321→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   322→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   323→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   324→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   325→	if path == "" {
   326→		return nil, 0, nil
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   330→	if readErr != nil {
   331→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   332→			return nil, 0, nil
   333→		}
   334→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	if len(data) == 0 {
   338→		return nil, 0, nil
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   342→	if parseErr != nil {
   343→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   344→	}
   345→
   346→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   347→
   348→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   349→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   350→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   351→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   352→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   353→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   354→			continue
   355→		}
   356→
   357→		// Process tool calls in this message
   358→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   359→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   360→			isModifyTool := false
   361→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   362→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   363→					isModifyTool = true
   364→					break
   365→				}
   366→			}
   367→
   368→			if !isModifyTool {
   369→				continue
   370→			}
   371→
   372→			// Extract file path from args map
   373→			var file string
   374→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   375→				file = fp
   376→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   377→				file = p
   378→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   379→				file = fn
   380→			}
   381→
   382→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   383→				fileSet[file] = true
   384→				files = append(files, file)
   385→			}
   386→		}
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   390→}
   391→
   392→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   393→
   394→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   395→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   396→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   397→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   398→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   399→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   400→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   401→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   402→		if chunkErr != nil {
   403→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   404→		}
   405→		return chunks, nil
   406→	}
   407→
   408→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   409→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	var chunks [][]byte
   413→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   414→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   415→
   416→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   417→		// Marshal message to get its size
   418→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   419→		if err != nil {
   420→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   421→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   422→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   423→			)
   424→			continue
   425→		}
   426→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   427→
   428→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   429→			// Save current chunk
   430→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   431→			if err != nil {
   432→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   433→			}
   434→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   435→
   436→			// Start new chunk
   437→			currentMessages = nil
   438→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   439→		}
   440→
   441→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   442→		currentSize += msgSize
   443→	}
   444→
   445→	// Add the last chunk
   446→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   447→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   448→		if err != nil {
   449→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   450→		}
   451→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   452→	}
   453→
   454→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   455→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   456→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	return chunks, nil
   460→}
   461→
   462→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   463→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   464→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   465→
   466→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   467→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   468→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   469→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   470→		}
   471→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   472→	}
   473→
   474→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   475→	if err != nil {
   476→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   477→	}
   478→	return result, nil
   479→}
