    25→
    26→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points.
    27→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListTemporaryCheckpoints for reading from shadow branches.
    28→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
    29→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	// Get checkpoint store
    35→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	// Get current HEAD to find matching shadow branch
    41→	head, err := repo.Head()
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Find sessions for current HEAD
    47→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(head.Hash().String())
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		// Log error but continue to check for logs-only points
    50→		sessions = nil
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	var allPoints []RewindPoint
    54→
    55→	// Collect checkpoint points from active sessions using checkpoint.GitStore
    56→	// Cache session prompts by session ID to avoid re-reading the same prompt file
    57→	sessionPrompts := make(map[string]string)
    58→
    59→	for _, state := range sessions {
    60→		checkpoints, err := store.ListTemporaryCheckpoints(context.Background(), state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID, state.SessionID, limit)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue // Error reading checkpoints, skip this session
    63→		}
    64→
    65→		for _, cp := range checkpoints {
    66→			// Get session prompt (cached by session ID)
    67→			sessionPrompt, ok := sessionPrompts[cp.SessionID]
    68→			if !ok {
    69→				sessionPrompt = readSessionPrompt(repo, cp.CommitHash, cp.MetadataDir)
    70→				sessionPrompts[cp.SessionID] = sessionPrompt
    71→			}
    72→
    73→			allPoints = append(allPoints, RewindPoint{
    74→				ID:               cp.CommitHash.String(),
    75→				Message:          cp.Message,
    76→				MetadataDir:      cp.MetadataDir,
    77→				Date:             cp.Timestamp,
    78→				IsTaskCheckpoint: cp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
    79→				ToolUseID:        cp.ToolUseID,
    80→				SessionID:        cp.SessionID,
    81→				SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
    82→				Agent:            state.AgentType,
    83→			})
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Sort by date, most recent first
    88→	sort.Slice(allPoints, func(i, j int) bool {
    89→		return allPoints[i].Date.After(allPoints[j].Date)
    90→	})
    91→
    92→	if len(allPoints) > limit {
    93→		allPoints = allPoints[:limit]
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Also include logs-only points from commit history
    97→	logsOnlyPoints, err := s.GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints(limit)
    98→	if err == nil && len(logsOnlyPoints) > 0 {
    99→		// Build set of existing point IDs for deduplication
   100→		existingIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   101→		for _, p := range allPoints {
   102→			existingIDs[p.ID] = true
   103→		}
   104→
   105→		// Add logs-only points that aren't already in the list
   106→		for _, p := range logsOnlyPoints {
   107→			if !existingIDs[p.ID] {
   108→				allPoints = append(allPoints, p)
   109→			}
   110→		}
   111→
   112→		// Re-sort by date
   113→		sort.Slice(allPoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   114→			return allPoints[i].Date.After(allPoints[j].Date)
   115→		})
   116→
   117→		// Re-trim to limit
   118→		if len(allPoints) > limit {
   119→			allPoints = allPoints[:limit]
   120→		}
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	return allPoints, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints finds commits in the current branch's history that have
   127→// condensed session logs on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch. These are commits that
   128→// were created with session data but the shadow branch has been condensed.
   129→//
   130→// The function works by:
   131→// 1. Getting all checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   132→// 2. Building a map of checkpoint ID -> checkpoint info
   133→// 3. Scanning the current branch history for commits with Entire-Checkpoint trailers
   134→// 4. Matching by checkpoint ID (stable across amend/rebase)
   135→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
   136→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   137→	if err != nil {
   138→		return nil, err
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Get all checkpoints from entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   142→	checkpoints, err := s.listCheckpoints()
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		// No checkpoints yet is fine
   145→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no checkpoints exist
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if len(checkpoints) == 0 {
   149→		return nil, nil
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	// Build map of checkpoint ID -> checkpoint info
   153→	// Checkpoint ID is the stable link from Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   154→	checkpointInfoMap := make(map[id.CheckpointID]CheckpointInfo)
   155→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   156→		if !cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   157→			checkpointInfoMap[cp.CheckpointID] = cp
   158→		}
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	// Get metadata branch tree for reading session prompts (best-effort, ignore errors)
   162→	metadataTree, _ := GetMetadataBranchTree(repo) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort for session prompts
   163→
   164→	head, err := repo.Head()
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Use LogOptions with Order=LogOrderCommitterTime to traverse all parents of merge commits.
   170→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   171→		From:  head.Hash(),
   172→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   173→	})
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	var points []RewindPoint
   179→	count := 0
   180→
   181→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   182→		if count >= logsOnlyScanLimit {
   183→			return errStop
   184→		}
   185→		count++
   186→
   187→		// Extract checkpoint ID from Entire-Checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   188→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   189→		if !found {
   190→			return nil
   191→		}
   192→		// Check if this checkpoint ID has metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1
   193→		cpInfo, found := checkpointInfoMap[cpID]
   194→		if !found {
   195→			return nil
   196→		}
   197→
   198→		// Create logs-only rewind point
   199→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   200→
   201→		// Read session prompts from metadata tree
   202→		var sessionPrompt string
   203→		var sessionPrompts []string
   204→		if metadataTree != nil {
   205→			checkpointPath := paths.CheckpointPath(cpInfo.CheckpointID) //nolint:staticcheck // already present in codebase
   206→			// For multi-session checkpoints, read all prompts
   207→			if cpInfo.SessionCount > 1 && len(cpInfo.SessionIDs) > 1 {
   208→				sessionPrompts = ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath, cpInfo.SessionCount, cpInfo.SessionIDs)
   209→				// Use the last (most recent) prompt as the main session prompt
   210→				if len(sessionPrompts) > 0 {
   211→					sessionPrompt = sessionPrompts[len(sessionPrompts)-1]
   212→				}
   213→			} else {
   214→				sessionPrompt = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   215→				if sessionPrompt != "" {
   216→					sessionPrompts = []string{sessionPrompt}
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→
   221→		points = append(points, RewindPoint{
   222→			ID:             c.Hash.String(),
   223→			Message:        message,
   224→			Date:           c.Author.When,
   225→			IsLogsOnly:     true,
   226→			CheckpointID:   cpInfo.CheckpointID,
   227→			Agent:          cpInfo.Agent,
   228→			SessionID:      cpInfo.SessionID,
   229→			SessionPrompt:  sessionPrompt,
   230→			SessionCount:   cpInfo.SessionCount,
   231→			SessionIDs:     cpInfo.SessionIDs,
   232→			SessionPrompts: sessionPrompts,
   233→		})
   234→
   235→		return nil
   236→	})
   237→
   238→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   239→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("error iterating commits: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	if len(points) > limit {
   243→		points = points[:limit]
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return points, nil
   247→}
   248→
   249→// Rewind restores the working directory to a checkpoint.
   250→//
   251→
   252→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
   253→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   254→	if err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Get the checkpoint commit
   259→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   260→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   266→	if err != nil {
   267→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	// Reset the shadow branch to the rewound checkpoint
   271→	// This ensures the next checkpoint will only include prompts from this point forward
   272→	if err := s.resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint(repo, commit); err != nil {
   273→		// Log warning but don't fail - file restoration is the primary operation
   274→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to reset shadow branch: %v\n", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Load session state to get untracked files that existed at session start
   278→	sessionID, hasSessionTrailer := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   279→	var preservedUntrackedFiles map[string]bool
   280→	if hasSessionTrailer {
   281→		state, stateErr := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   282→		if stateErr == nil && state != nil && len(state.UntrackedFilesAtStart) > 0 {
   283→			preservedUntrackedFiles = make(map[string]bool)
   284→			for _, f := range state.UntrackedFilesAtStart {
   285→				preservedUntrackedFiles[f] = true
   286→			}
   287→		}
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Build set of files in the checkpoint tree (excluding metadata)
   291→	checkpointFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   292→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   293→		if !strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   294→			checkpointFiles[f.Name] = true
   295→		}
   296→		return nil
   297→	})
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoint files: %w", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// Get HEAD tree to identify tracked files
   303→	head, err := repo.Head()
   304→	if err != nil {
   305→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   306→	}
   307→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD tree: %w", err)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	// Build set of files tracked in HEAD
   317→	trackedFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   318→	//nolint:errcheck // Error is not critical for rewind
   319→	_ = headTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   320→		trackedFiles[f.Name] = true
   321→		return nil
   322→	})
   323→
   324→	// Get repository root to walk from there
   325→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   326→	if err != nil {
   327→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to current directory
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	// Find and delete untracked files that aren't in the checkpoint
   331→	// These are likely files created by the agent in later checkpoints
   332→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
   333→		if walkErr != nil {
   334→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		// Get path relative to repo root
   338→		relPath, relErr := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
   339→		if relErr != nil {
   340→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Skip directories and protected paths
   344→		if info.IsDir() {
   345→			if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   346→				return filepath.SkipDir
   347→			}
   348→			return nil
   349→		}
   350→
   351→		// Skip files in protected directories
   352→		if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   353→			return nil
   354→		}
   355→
   356→		// If file is in checkpoint, it will be restored
   357→		if checkpointFiles[relPath] {
   358→			return nil
   359→		}
   360→
   361→		// If file is tracked in HEAD, don't delete (user's committed work)
   362→		if trackedFiles[relPath] {
   363→			return nil
   364→		}
   365→
   366→		// If file existed at session start, preserve it (untracked user files)
   367→		if preservedUntrackedFiles[relPath] {
   368→			return nil
   369→		}
   370→
   371→		// File is untracked and not in checkpoint - delete it (use absolute path)
   372→		if removeErr := os.Remove(path); removeErr == nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Deleted: %s\n", relPath)
   374→		}
   375→
   376→		return nil
   377→	})
   378→	if err != nil {
   379→		// Non-fatal - continue with restoration
   380→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: error walking directory: %v\n", err)
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	// Restore files from checkpoint
   384→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   385→		// Skip metadata directories - these are for checkpoint storage, not working dir
   386→		if strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   387→			return nil
   388→		}
   389→
   390→		contents, err := f.Contents()
   391→		if err != nil {
   392→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to read file %s: %w", f.Name, err)
   393→		}
   394→
   395→		// Ensure directory exists
   396→		dir := filepath.Dir(f.Name)
   397→		if dir != "." {
   398→			//nolint:gosec // G301: Need 0o755 for user directories during rewind
   399→			if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   400→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory %s: %w", dir, err)
   401→			}
   402→		}
   403→
   404→		// Write file with appropriate permissions
   405→		var perm os.FileMode = 0o644
   406→		if f.Mode == filemode.Executable {
   407→			perm = 0o755
   408→		}
   409→		if err := os.WriteFile(f.Name, []byte(contents), perm); err != nil {
   410→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write file %s: %w", f.Name, err)
   411→		}
   412→
   413→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Restored: %s\n", f.Name)
   414→		return nil
   415→	})
   416→	if err != nil {
   417→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate tree files: %w", err)
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	fmt.Println()
   421→	if len(point.ID) >= 7 {
   422→		fmt.Printf("Restored files from shadow commit %s\n", point.ID[:7])
   423→	} else {
   424→		fmt.Printf("Restored files from shadow commit %s\n", point.ID)
   425→	}
   426→	fmt.Println()
   427→
   428→	return nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint resets the shadow branch HEAD to the given checkpoint.
   432→// This ensures that when the user commits after rewinding, the next checkpoint will only
   433→// include prompts from the rewound point, not prompts from later checkpoints.
   434→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint(repo *git.Repository, commit *object.Commit) error {
   435→	// Extract session ID from the checkpoint commit's Entire-Session trailer
   436→	sessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   437→	if !found {
   438→		return errors.New("checkpoint has no Entire-Session trailer")
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Load session state to get the shadow branch name
   442→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→	if state == nil {
   447→		return fmt.Errorf("session %s not found", sessionID)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	// Reset the shadow branch to the checkpoint commit
   451→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   452→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   453→
   454→	// Update the reference to point to the checkpoint commit
   455→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commit.Hash)
   456→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   457→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update shadow branch: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Reset shadow branch %s to checkpoint %s\n", shadowBranchName, commit.Hash.String()[:7])
   461→	return nil
   462→}
   463→
   464→// CanRewind checks if rewinding is possible.
   465→// For manual-commit strategy, rewind restores files from a checkpoint - uncommitted changes are expected
   466→// and will be replaced by the checkpoint contents. Returns true with a warning message showing
   467→// what changes will be reverted.
   468→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   469→	return checkCanRewindWithWarning()
   470→}
   471→
   472→// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   473→// This allows showing warnings about untracked files that will be deleted.
   474→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   475→	// Logs-only points don't modify the working directory
   476→	if point.IsLogsOnly {
   477→		return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   481→	if err != nil {
   482→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Get the checkpoint commit
   486→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   487→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   488→	if err != nil {
   489→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   493→	if err != nil {
   494→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	// Load session state to get untracked files that existed at session start
   498→	sessionID, hasSessionTrailer := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   499→	var preservedUntrackedFiles map[string]bool
   500→	if hasSessionTrailer {
   501→		state, stateErr := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   502→		if stateErr == nil && state != nil && len(state.UntrackedFilesAtStart) > 0 {
   503→			preservedUntrackedFiles = make(map[string]bool)
   504→			for _, f := range state.UntrackedFilesAtStart {
   505→				preservedUntrackedFiles[f] = true
   506→			}
   507→		}
   508→	}
   509→
   510→	// Build set of files in the checkpoint tree (excluding metadata)
   511→	checkpointFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   512→	var filesToRestore []string
   513→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   514→		if !strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   515→			checkpointFiles[f.Name] = true
   516→			filesToRestore = append(filesToRestore, f.Name)
   517→		}
   518→		return nil
   519→	})
   520→	if err != nil {
   521→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoint files: %w", err)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Get HEAD tree to identify tracked files
   525→	head, err := repo.Head()
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   530→	if err != nil {
   531→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   532→	}
   533→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   534→	if err != nil {
   535→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD tree: %w", err)
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	// Build set of files tracked in HEAD
   539→	trackedFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   540→	//nolint:errcheck // Error is not critical for preview
   541→	_ = headTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   542→		trackedFiles[f.Name] = true
   543→		return nil
   544→	})
   545→
   546→	// Get repository root to walk from there
   547→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   548→	if err != nil {
   549→		repoRoot = "."
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Find untracked files that would be deleted
   553→	var filesToDelete []string
   554→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
   555→		if walkErr != nil {
   556→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
   557→		}
   558→
   559→		relPath, relErr := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
   560→		if relErr != nil {
   561→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
   562→		}
   563→
   564→		// Skip directories and protected paths
   565→		if info.IsDir() {
   566→			if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   567→				return filepath.SkipDir
   568→			}
   569→			return nil
   570→		}
   571→
   572→		// Skip files in protected directories
   573→		if isProtectedPath(relPath) {
   574→			return nil
   575→		}
   576→
   577→		// If file is in checkpoint, it will be restored (not deleted)
   578→		if checkpointFiles[relPath] {
   579→			return nil
   580→		}
   581→
   582→		// If file is tracked in HEAD, don't delete (user's committed work)
   583→		if trackedFiles[relPath] {
   584→			return nil
   585→		}
   586→
   587→		// If file existed at session start, preserve it (untracked user files)
   588→		if preservedUntrackedFiles[relPath] {
   589→			return nil
   590→		}
   591→
   592→		// File is untracked and not in checkpoint - will be deleted
   593→		filesToDelete = append(filesToDelete, relPath)
   594→		return nil
   595→	})
   596→	if err != nil {
   597→		// Non-fatal, return what we have
   598→		return &RewindPreview{ //nolint:nilerr // Partial result is still useful
   599→			FilesToRestore: filesToRestore,
   600→			FilesToDelete:  filesToDelete,
   601→		}, nil
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	// Sort for consistent output
   605→	sort.Strings(filesToRestore)
   606→	sort.Strings(filesToDelete)
   607→
   608→	return &RewindPreview{
   609→		FilesToRestore: filesToRestore,
   610→		FilesToDelete:  filesToDelete,
   611→	}, nil
   612→}
   613→
   614→// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   615→// This fetches the transcript from entire/checkpoints/v1 and writes it to the agent's session directory.
   616→// Does not modify the working directory.
   617→// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.
   618→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   619→// Returns info about each restored session so callers can print correct per-session resume commands.
   620→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error) {
   621→	if !point.IsLogsOnly {
   622→		return nil, errors.New("not a logs-only rewind point")
   623→	}
   624→
   625→	if point.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   626→		return nil, errors.New("missing checkpoint ID")
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Get checkpoint store
   630→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   631→	if err != nil {
   632→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	// Read checkpoint summary to get session count
   636→	summary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)
   637→	if err != nil {
   638→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   639→	}
   640→	if summary == nil {
   641→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", point.CheckpointID)
   642→	}
   643→
   644→	// Get repo root for agent session directory lookup
   645→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   646→	if err != nil {
   647→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   648→	}
   649→
   650→	// Check for newer local logs if not forcing
   651→	if !force {
   652→		sessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), repoRoot, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)
   653→		hasConflicts := false
   654→		for _, sess := range sessions {
   655→			if sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   656→				hasConflicts = true
   657→				break
   658→			}
   659→		}
   660→		if hasConflicts {
   661→			shouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)
   662→			if promptErr != nil {
   663→				return nil, promptErr
   664→			}
   665→			if !shouldOverwrite {
   666→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resume cancelled. Local session logs preserved.\n")
   667→				return nil, nil
   668→			}
   669→		}
   670→	}
   671→
   672→	// Count sessions to restore
   673→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   674→	if totalSessions > 1 {
   675→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\n", totalSessions)
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Restore all sessions (oldest to newest, using 0-based indexing)
   679→	var restored []RestoredSession
   680→	for i := range totalSessions {
   681→		content, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID, i)
   682→		if readErr != nil {
   683→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: failed to read session %d: %v\n", i, readErr)
   684→			continue
   685→		}
   686→		if content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   687→			continue
   688→		}
   689→
   690→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   691→		if sessionID == "" {
   692→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: session %d has no session ID, skipping\n", i)
   693→			continue
   694→		}
   695→
   696→		// Resolve per-session agent from metadata — skip if agent is unknown
   697→		if content.Metadata.Agent == "" {
   698→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: session %d (%s) has no agent metadata, skipping (cannot determine target directory)\n", i, sessionID)
   699→			continue
   700→		}
   701→		sessionAgent, agErr := ResolveAgentForRewind(content.Metadata.Agent)
   702→		if agErr != nil {
   703→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: session %d (%s) has unknown agent %q, skipping\n", i, sessionID, content.Metadata.Agent)
   704→			continue
   705→		}
   706→
   707→		sessionAgentDir, dirErr := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   708→		if dirErr != nil {
   709→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: failed to get session dir for session %d: %v\n", i, dirErr)
   710→			continue
   711→		}
   712→
   713→		sessionFile := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)
   714→
   715→		// Get first prompt for display
   716→		promptPreview := ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts)
   717→
   718→		if totalSessions > 1 {
   719→			isLatest := i == totalSessions-1
   720→			if promptPreview != "" {
   721→				if isLatest {
   722→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d (latest): %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   723→				} else {
   724→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d: %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   725→				}
   726→			}
   727→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Writing to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   728→		} else {
   729→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing transcript to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   730→		}
   731→
   732→		// Ensure parent directory exists (session file may be in a different dir than sessionAgentDir)
   733→		if mkdirErr := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); mkdirErr != nil {
   734→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Warning: failed to create directory: %v\n", mkdirErr)
   735→			continue
   736→		}
   737→
   738→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(sessionFile, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   739→			if totalSessions > 1 {
   740→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Warning: failed to write transcript: %v\n", writeErr)
   741→				continue
   742→			}
   743→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", writeErr)
   744→		}
   745→
   746→		restored = append(restored, RestoredSession{
   747→			SessionID: sessionID,
   748→			Agent:     sessionAgent.Type(),
   749→			Prompt:    promptPreview,
   750→		})
   751→	}
   752→
   753→	return restored, nil
   754→}
   755→
   756→// ResolveAgentForRewind resolves the agent from checkpoint metadata.
   757→// Falls back to the default agent (Claude) for old checkpoints that lack agent info.
   758→func ResolveAgentForRewind(agentType agent.AgentType) (agent.Agent, error) {
   759→	if !isSpecificAgentType(agentType) {
   760→		ag := agent.Default()
   761→		if ag == nil {
   762→			return nil, errors.New("no default agent registered")
   763→		}
   764→		return ag, nil
   765→	}
   766→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
   767→	if err != nil {
   768→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolving agent %q: %w", agentType, err)
   769→	}
   770→	return ag, nil
   771→}
   772→
   773→// ResolveSessionFilePath determines the correct file path for an agent's session transcript.
   774→// Checks session state for transcript_path first (needed for agents like Gemini that store
   775→// transcripts at paths that GetSessionDir can't reconstruct, e.g. SHA-256 hashed directories).
   776→// Falls back to the agent's ExtractAgentSessionID + ResolveSessionFile with fallbackSessionDir.
   777→func ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID string, ag agent.Agent, fallbackSessionDir string) string {
   778→	state, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   779→	if err == nil && state != nil && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   780→		return state.TranscriptPath
   781→	}
   782→
   783→	agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   784→	return ag.ResolveSessionFile(fallbackSessionDir, agentSessionID)
   785→}
   786→
   787→// readSessionPrompt reads the first prompt from the session's prompt.txt file stored in git.
   788→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   789→func readSessionPrompt(repo *git.Repository, commitHash plumbing.Hash, metadataDir string) string {
   790→	// Get the commit and its tree
   791→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   792→	if err != nil {
   793→		return ""
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   797→	if err != nil {
   798→		return ""
   799→	}
   800→
   801→	// Look for prompt.txt in the metadata directory
   802→	promptPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.PromptFileName
   803→	promptEntry, err := tree.File(promptPath)
   804→	if err != nil {
   805→		return ""
   806→	}
   807→
   808→	content, err := promptEntry.Contents()
   809→	if err != nil {
   810→		return ""
   811→	}
   812→
   813→	return ExtractFirstPrompt(content)
   814→}
   815→
   816→// SessionRestoreStatus represents the status of a session being restored.
   817→type SessionRestoreStatus int
   818→
   819→const (
   820→	StatusNew             SessionRestoreStatus = iota // Local file doesn't exist
   821→	StatusUnchanged                                   // Local and checkpoint are the same
   822→	StatusCheckpointNewer                             // Checkpoint has newer entries
   823→	StatusLocalNewer                                  // Local has newer entries (conflict)
   824→)
   825→
   826→// SessionRestoreInfo contains information about a session being restored.
   827→type SessionRestoreInfo struct {
   828→	SessionID      string
   829→	Prompt         string               // First prompt preview for display
   830→	Status         SessionRestoreStatus // Status of this session
   831→	LocalTime      time.Time
   832→	CheckpointTime time.Time
   833→}
   834→
   835→// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info
   836→// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.
   837→// repoRoot is used to compute per-session agent directories.
   838→// Sessions without agent metadata are skipped (cannot determine target directory).
   839→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {
   840→	var sessions []SessionRestoreInfo
   841→
   842→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   843→	// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)
   844→	for i := range totalSessions {
   845→		content, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   846→		if err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   847→			continue
   848→		}
   849→
   850→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   851→		if sessionID == "" || content.Metadata.Agent == "" {
   852→			continue
   853→		}
   854→
   855→		sessionAgent, agErr := ResolveAgentForRewind(content.Metadata.Agent)
   856→		if agErr != nil {
   857→			continue
   858→		}
   859→
   860→		sessionAgentDir, dirErr := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   861→		if dirErr != nil {
   862→			continue
   863→		}
   864→
   865→		localPath := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)
   866→
   867→		localTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)
   868→		checkpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)
   869→		status := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)
   870→
   871→		sessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{
   872→			SessionID:      sessionID,
   873→			Prompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),
   874→			Status:         status,
   875→			LocalTime:      localTime,
   876→			CheckpointTime: checkpointTime,
   877→		})
   878→	}
   879→
   880→	return sessions
   881→}
   882→
   883→// ClassifyTimestamps determines the restore status based on local and checkpoint timestamps.
   884→func ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime time.Time) SessionRestoreStatus {
   885→	// Local file doesn't exist (no timestamp found)
   886→	if localTime.IsZero() {
   887→		return StatusNew
   888→	}
   889→
   890→	// Can't determine checkpoint time - treat as new/safe
   891→	if checkpointTime.IsZero() {
   892→		return StatusNew
   893→	}
   894→
   895→	// Compare timestamps
   896→	if localTime.After(checkpointTime) {
   897→		return StatusLocalNewer
   898→	}
   899→	if checkpointTime.After(localTime) {
   900→		return StatusCheckpointNewer
   901→	}
   902→	return StatusUnchanged
   903→}
   904→
   905→// StatusToText returns a human-readable status string.
   906→func StatusToText(status SessionRestoreStatus) string {
   907→	switch status {
   908→	case StatusNew:
   909→		return "(new)"
   910→	case StatusUnchanged:
   911→		return "(unchanged)"
   912→	case StatusCheckpointNewer:
   913→		return "(checkpoint is newer)"
   914→	case StatusLocalNewer:
   915→		return "(local is newer)" // shouldn't appear in non-conflict list
   916→	default:
   917→		return ""
   918→	}
   919→}
   920→
   921→// PromptOverwriteNewerLogs asks the user for confirmation to overwrite local
   922→// session logs that have newer timestamps than the checkpoint versions.
   923→func PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions []SessionRestoreInfo) (bool, error) {
   924→	// Separate conflicting and non-conflicting sessions
   925→	var conflicting, nonConflicting []SessionRestoreInfo
   926→	for _, s := range sessions {
   927→		if s.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   928→			conflicting = append(conflicting, s)
   929→		} else {
   930→			nonConflicting = append(nonConflicting, s)
   931→		}
   932→	}
   933→
   934→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: Local session log(s) have newer entries than the checkpoint:\n")
   935→	for _, info := range conflicting {
   936→		// Show prompt if available, otherwise fall back to session ID
   937→		if info.Prompt != "" {
   938→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  \"%s\"\n", info.Prompt)
   939→		} else {
   940→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session: %s\n", info.SessionID)
   941→		}
   942→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Local last entry:      %s\n", info.LocalTime.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   943→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Checkpoint last entry: %s\n", info.CheckpointTime.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   944→	}
   945→
   946→	// Show non-conflicting sessions with their status
   947→	if len(nonConflicting) > 0 {
   948→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nThese other session(s) will also be restored:\n")
   949→		for _, info := range nonConflicting {
   950→			statusText := StatusToText(info.Status)
   951→			if info.Prompt != "" {
   952→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  \"%s\" %s\n", info.Prompt, statusText)
   953→			} else {
   954→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session: %s %s\n", info.SessionID, statusText)
   955→			}
   956→		}
   957→	}
   958→
   959→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nOverwriting will lose the newer local entries.\n\n")
   960→
   961→	var confirmed bool
   962→	form := huh.NewForm(
   963→		huh.NewGroup(
   964→			huh.NewConfirm().
   965→				Title("Overwrite local session logs with checkpoint versions?").
   966→				Value(&confirmed),
   967→		),
   968→	)
   969→	if isAccessibleMode() {
   970→		form = form.WithAccessible(true)
   971→	}
   972→
   973→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   974→		if errors.Is(err, huh.ErrUserAborted) {
   975→			return false, nil
   976→		}
   977→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get confirmation: %w", err)
   978→	}
   979→
   980→	return confirmed, nil
   981→}
   982→

     1→// Package checkpoint provides types and interfaces for checkpoint storage.
     2→//
     3→// A Checkpoint captures a point-in-time within a session, containing either
     4→// full state (Temporary) or metadata with a commit reference (Committed).
     5→//
     6→// See docs/architecture/sessions-and-checkpoints.md for the full domain model.
     7→package checkpoint
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"context"
    11→	"errors"
    12→	"time"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    16→
    17→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    18→)
    19→
    20→// Errors returned by checkpoint operations.
    21→var (
    22→	// ErrCheckpointNotFound is returned when a checkpoint ID doesn't exist.
    23→	ErrCheckpointNotFound = errors.New("checkpoint not found")
    24→
    25→	// ErrNoTranscript is returned when a checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
    26→	ErrNoTranscript = errors.New("no transcript found for checkpoint")
    27→)
    28→
    29→// Checkpoint represents a save point within a session.
    30→type Checkpoint struct {
    31→	// ID is the unique checkpoint identifier
    32→	ID string
    33→
    34→	// SessionID is the session this checkpoint belongs to
    35→	SessionID string
    36→
    37→	// Timestamp is when this checkpoint was created
    38→	Timestamp time.Time
    39→
    40→	// Type indicates temporary (full state) or committed (metadata only)
    41→	Type Type
    42→
    43→	// Message is a human-readable description of the checkpoint
    44→	Message string
    45→}
    46→
    47→// Type indicates the storage location and lifecycle of a checkpoint.
    48→type Type int
    49→
    50→const (
    51→	// Temporary checkpoints contain full state (code + metadata) and are stored
    52→	// on shadow branches (entire/<commit-hash>). Used for intra-session rewind.
    53→	Temporary Type = iota
    54→
    55→	// Committed checkpoints contain metadata + commit reference and are stored
    56→	// on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch. They are the permanent record.
    57→	Committed
    58→)
    59→
    60→// Store provides low-level primitives for reading and writing checkpoints.
    61→// This is used by strategies to implement their storage approach.
    62→//
    63→// The interface matches the GitStore implementation signatures directly:
    64→// - WriteTemporary takes WriteTemporaryOptions and returns a result with commit hash and skip status
    65→// - ReadTemporary takes baseCommit (not sessionID) since shadow branches are keyed by commit
    66→// - List methods return implementation-specific info types for richer data
    67→type Store interface {
    68→	// WriteTemporary writes a temporary checkpoint (full state) to a shadow branch.
    69→	// Shadow branches are named entire/<base-commit-short-hash>.
    70→	// Returns a result containing the commit hash and whether the checkpoint was skipped.
    71→	// Checkpoints are skipped (deduplicated) when the tree hash matches the previous checkpoint.
    72→	WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error)
    73→
    74→	// ReadTemporary reads the latest checkpoint from a shadow branch.
    75→	// baseCommit is the commit hash the session is based on.
    76→	// worktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree).
    77→	// Returns nil, nil if the shadow branch doesn't exist.
    78→	ReadTemporary(ctx context.Context, baseCommit, worktreeID string) (*ReadTemporaryResult, error)
    79→
    80→	// ListTemporary lists all shadow branches with their checkpoint info.
    81→	ListTemporary(ctx context.Context) ([]TemporaryInfo, error)
    82→
    83→	// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    84→	// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    85→	WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error
    86→
    87→	// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID.
    88→	// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
    89→	// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
    90→	// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint does not exist.
    91→	ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error)
    92→
    93→	// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
    94→	// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
    95→	// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
    96→	ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error)
    97→
    98→	// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
    99→	// Useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   100→	ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error)
   101→
   102→	// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints.
   103→	ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error)
   104→}
   105→
   106→// WriteTemporaryResult contains the result of writing a temporary checkpoint.
   107→type WriteTemporaryResult struct {
   108→	// CommitHash is the hash of the created or existing checkpoint commit
   109→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   110→
   111→	// Skipped is true if the checkpoint was skipped due to no changes
   112→	// (tree hash matched the previous checkpoint)
   113→	Skipped bool
   114→}

   257→
   258→	// Transcript position at checkpoint start - tracks what was added during this checkpoint
   259→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string // Last identifier when checkpoint started (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini)
   260→	CheckpointTranscriptStart   int    // Transcript line offset at start of this checkpoint's data
   261→
   262→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart is written to both CommittedMetadata.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   263→	// and the deprecated CommittedMetadata.TranscriptLinesAtStart for backward compatibility.
   264→
   265→	// TokenUsage contains the token usage for this checkpoint
   266→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   267→
   268→	// InitialAttribution is line-level attribution calculated at commit time
   269→	// comparing checkpoint tree (agent work) to committed tree (may include human edits)
   270→	InitialAttribution *InitialAttribution
   271→
   272→	// Summary is an optional AI-generated summary for this checkpoint.
   273→	// This field may be nil when:
   274→	//   - summarization is disabled in settings
   275→	//   - summary generation failed (non-blocking, logged as warning)
   276→	//   - the transcript was empty or too short to summarize
   277→	//   - the checkpoint predates the summarization feature
   278→	Summary *Summary
   279→}
   280→
   281→// CommittedInfo contains summary information about a committed checkpoint.
   282→type CommittedInfo struct {
   283→	// CheckpointID is the stable 12-hex-char identifier
   284→	CheckpointID id.CheckpointID
   285→
   286→	// SessionID is the session identifier (most recent session for multi-session checkpoints)
   287→	SessionID string
   288→
   289→	// CreatedAt is when the checkpoint was created
   290→	CreatedAt time.Time
   291→
   292→	// CheckpointsCount is the total number of checkpoints across all sessions
   293→	CheckpointsCount int
   294→
   295→	// FilesTouched are files modified during all sessions
   296→	FilesTouched []string
   297→
   298→	// Agent identifies the agent that created this checkpoint
   299→	Agent agent.AgentType
   300→
   301→	// IsTask indicates if this is a task checkpoint
   302→	IsTask bool
   303→
   304→	// ToolUseID is the tool use ID for task checkpoints
   305→	ToolUseID string
   306→
   307→	// Multi-session support
   308→	SessionCount int      // Number of sessions (1 if single session)
   309→	SessionIDs   []string // All session IDs that contributed
   310→}
   311→
   312→// SessionContent contains the actual content for a session.
   313→// This is used when reading full session data (transcript, prompts, context)
   314→// as opposed to just the metadata/summary.
   315→type SessionContent struct {
   316→	// Metadata contains the session-specific metadata
   317→	Metadata CommittedMetadata
   318→
   319→	// Transcript is the session transcript content
   320→	Transcript []byte
   321→
   322→	// Prompts contains user prompts from this session
   323→	Prompts string
   324→
   325→	// Context is the context.md content
   326→	Context string
   327→}
   328→
   329→// CommittedMetadata contains the metadata stored in metadata.json for each checkpoint.
   330→type CommittedMetadata struct {
   331→	CLIVersion       string          `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
   332→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID `json:"checkpoint_id"`
   333→	SessionID        string          `json:"session_id"`
   334→	Strategy         string          `json:"strategy"`
   335→	CreatedAt        time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
   336→	Branch           string          `json:"branch,omitempty"` // Branch where checkpoint was created (empty if detached HEAD)
   337→	CheckpointsCount int             `json:"checkpoints_count"`
   338→	FilesTouched     []string        `json:"files_touched"`
   339→
   340→	// Agent identifies the agent that created this checkpoint (e.g., "Claude Code", "Cursor")
   341→	Agent agent.AgentType `json:"agent,omitempty"`
   342→
   343→	// Task checkpoint fields (only populated for task checkpoints)
   344→	IsTask    bool   `json:"is_task,omitempty"`
   345→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
   346→
   347→	// Transcript position at checkpoint start - tracks what was added during this checkpoint
   348→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"` // Last identifier when checkpoint started (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini)
   349→	CheckpointTranscriptStart   int    `json:"checkpoint_transcript_start,omitempty"`    // Transcript line offset at start of this checkpoint's data
   350→
   351→	// Deprecated: Use CheckpointTranscriptStart instead. Written for backward compatibility with older CLI versions.
   352→	TranscriptLinesAtStart int `json:"transcript_lines_at_start,omitempty"`
   353→
   354→	// Token usage for this checkpoint
   355→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
   356→
   357→	// AI-generated summary of the checkpoint
   358→	Summary *Summary `json:"summary,omitempty"`
   359→
   360→	// InitialAttribution is line-level attribution calculated at commit time
   361→	InitialAttribution *InitialAttribution `json:"initial_attribution,omitempty"`
   362→}
   363→
   364→// GetTranscriptStart returns the transcript line offset at which this checkpoint's data begins.
   365→// Returns 0 for new checkpoints (start from beginning). For data written by older CLI versions,
   366→// falls back to the deprecated TranscriptLinesAtStart field.
   367→func (m CommittedMetadata) GetTranscriptStart() int {
   368→	if m.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   369→		return m.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   370→	}
   371→	return m.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   372→}
   373→
   374→// SessionFilePaths contains the absolute paths to session files from the git tree root.
   375→// Paths include the full checkpoint path prefix (e.g., "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/1/metadata.json").
   376→// Used in CheckpointSummary.Sessions to map session IDs to their file locations.
   377→type SessionFilePaths struct {
   378→	Metadata    string `json:"metadata"`
   379→	Transcript  string `json:"transcript"`
   380→	Context     string `json:"context"`
   381→	ContentHash string `json:"content_hash"`
   382→	Prompt      string `json:"prompt"`
   383→}
   384→
   385→// CheckpointSummary is the root-level metadata.json for a checkpoint.
   386→// It contains aggregated statistics from all sessions and a map of session IDs
   387→// to their file paths. Session-specific data (including initial_attribution)
   388→// is stored in the session's subdirectory metadata.json.
   389→//
   390→// Structure on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch:
   391→//
   392→//	<checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/
   393→//	├── metadata.json         # This CheckpointSummary
   394→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   395→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # Session-specific CommittedMetadata
   396→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   397→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   398→//	│   ├── context.md
   399→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   400→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   401→//	└── 3/                    # Third session...
   402→//
   403→//nolint:revive // Named CheckpointSummary to avoid conflict with existing Summary struct
   404→type CheckpointSummary struct {
   405→	CLIVersion       string             `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
   406→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID    `json:"checkpoint_id"`
   407→	Strategy         string             `json:"strategy"`
   408→	Branch           string             `json:"branch,omitempty"`
   409→	CheckpointsCount int                `json:"checkpoints_count"`
   410→	FilesTouched     []string           `json:"files_touched"`
   411→	Sessions         []SessionFilePaths `json:"sessions"`
   412→	TokenUsage       *agent.TokenUsage  `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
   413→}
   414→
   415→// Summary contains AI-generated summary of a checkpoint.
   416→type Summary struct {
   417→	Intent    string           `json:"intent"`     // What user wanted to accomplish
   418→	Outcome   string           `json:"outcome"`    // What was achieved
   419→	Learnings LearningsSummary `json:"learnings"`  // Categorized learnings
   420→	Friction  []string         `json:"friction"`   // Problems/annoyances encountered
   421→	OpenItems []string         `json:"open_items"` // Tech debt, unfinished work
   422→}
   423→
   424→// LearningsSummary contains learnings grouped by scope.
   425→type LearningsSummary struct {
   426→	Repo     []string       `json:"repo"`     // Codebase-specific patterns/conventions
   427→	Code     []CodeLearning `json:"code"`     // File/module specific findings
   428→	Workflow []string       `json:"workflow"` // General dev practices
   429→}
   430→
   431→// CodeLearning captures a learning tied to a specific code location.
   432→type CodeLearning struct {
   433→	Path    string `json:"path"`               // File path
   434→	Line    int    `json:"line,omitempty"`     // Start line number
   435→	EndLine int    `json:"end_line,omitempty"` // End line for ranges (optional)
   436→	Finding string `json:"finding"`            // What was learned
   437→}
   438→
   439→// InitialAttribution captures line-level attribution metrics at commit time.
   440→// This is a point-in-time snapshot comparing the checkpoint tree (agent work)
   441→// against the committed tree (may include human edits).
   442→//
   443→// Attribution Metrics:
   444→//   - TotalCommitted measures "net additions" (lines added that remain in the commit)
   445→//   - AgentPercentage represents "of the new code added, what percentage came from the agent"
   446→//   - Deletion work is tracked separately in HumanRemoved (not included in percentage)
   447→//
   448→// Deletion-Only Commits:
   449→// For commits with only deletions (no additions), TotalCommitted will be 0 and
   450→// AgentPercentage will be 0. This is by design - the percentage metric is only
   451→// meaningful for commits that add code. Deletion contributions are captured in
   452→// the HumanRemoved field but don't affect the attribution percentage.
   453→type InitialAttribution struct {
   454→	CalculatedAt    time.Time `json:"calculated_at"`
   455→	AgentLines      int       `json:"agent_lines"`      // Lines added by agent (base → shadow diff)
   456→	HumanAdded      int       `json:"human_added"`      // Lines added by human (excluding modifications)
   457→	HumanModified   int       `json:"human_modified"`   // Lines modified by human (estimate: min(added, removed))
   458→	HumanRemoved    int       `json:"human_removed"`    // Lines removed by human (excluding modifications)
   459→	TotalCommitted  int       `json:"total_committed"`  // Net additions in commit (agent + human new lines, not total file size)
   460→	AgentPercentage float64   `json:"agent_percentage"` // agent_lines / total_committed * 100 (0 for deletion-only commits)
   461→}
   462→
   463→// Info provides summary information for listing checkpoints.
   464→// This is the generic checkpoint info type.
   465→type Info struct {
   466→	// ID is the checkpoint identifier
   467→	ID string
   468→
   469→	// SessionID identifies the session
   470→	SessionID string
   471→
   472→	// Type indicates temporary or committed
   473→	Type Type
   474→
   475→	// CreatedAt is when the checkpoint was created
   476→	CreatedAt time.Time
   477→
   478→	// Message is a summary description
   479→	Message string
   480→}
   481→
   482→// WriteTemporaryTaskOptions contains options for writing a task checkpoint.
   483→// Task checkpoints are created when a subagent completes and contain both
   484→// code changes and task-specific metadata.
   485→type WriteTemporaryTaskOptions struct {
   486→	// SessionID is the session identifier
   487→	SessionID string
   488→
   489→	// BaseCommit is the commit hash this session is based on
   490→	BaseCommit string
   491→
   492→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
   493→	// Used to create worktree-specific shadow branch names
   494→	WorktreeID string
   495→
   496→	// ToolUseID is the unique identifier for this Task tool invocation
   497→	ToolUseID string
   498→
   499→	// AgentID is the subagent identifier
   500→	AgentID string
   501→
   502→	// ModifiedFiles are files that have been modified (relative paths)
   503→	ModifiedFiles []string
   504→
   505→	// NewFiles are files that have been created (relative paths)
   506→	NewFiles []string
   507→
   508→	// DeletedFiles are files that have been deleted (relative paths)
   509→	DeletedFiles []string
   510→
   511→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the main session transcript
   512→	TranscriptPath string
   513→
   514→	// SubagentTranscriptPath is the path to the subagent's transcript
   515→	SubagentTranscriptPath string
   516→
   517→	// CheckpointUUID is the UUID for transcript truncation when rewinding
   518→	CheckpointUUID string
   519→
   520→	// CommitMessage is the commit message (already formatted)
   521→	CommitMessage string
   522→
   523→	// AuthorName is the name to use for commits
   524→	AuthorName string
   525→
   526→	// AuthorEmail is the email to use for commits
   527→	AuthorEmail string
   528→
   529→	// IsIncremental indicates this is an incremental checkpoint
   530→	IsIncremental bool
   531→
   532→	// IncrementalSequence is the checkpoint sequence number
   533→	IncrementalSequence int
   534→
   535→	// IncrementalType is the tool that triggered this checkpoint
   536→	IncrementalType string
   537→
   538→	// IncrementalData is the tool_input payload for this checkpoint
   539→	IncrementalData []byte
   540→}
   541→
   542→// TemporaryCheckpointInfo contains information about a single commit on a shadow branch.
   543→// Used by ListTemporaryCheckpoints to provide rewind point data.
   544→type TemporaryCheckpointInfo struct {
   545→	// CommitHash is the hash of the checkpoint commit
   546→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   547→
   548→	// Message is the first line of the commit message
   549→	Message string
   550→
   551→	// SessionID is the session identifier from the Entire-Session trailer
   552→	SessionID string

   554→	// MetadataDir is the metadata directory path from trailers

   556→
   557→	// IsTaskCheckpoint indicates if this is a task checkpoint

   560→	// ToolUseID is the tool use ID for task checkpoints
   561→	ToolUseID string
   562→
   563→	// Timestamp is when the checkpoint was created
   564→	Timestamp time.Time

    63→// parseGeminiSessionEnd parses the session-end hook input and validates transcript.
    64→func parseGeminiSessionEnd() (*geminiSessionContext, error) {
    65→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    76→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
    77→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
    78→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    79→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    80→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    81→	)
    82→
    83→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    84→	if sessionID == "" {
    85→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
    89→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	return &geminiSessionContext{
    94→		sessionID:      sessionID,
    95→		transcriptPath: transcriptPath,
    96→	}, nil
    97→}

    11→// Transcript parsing types - Gemini CLI uses JSON format for session storage

    23→}
    24→
    25→// GeminiMessage represents a single message in the transcript
    26→type GeminiMessage struct {
    27→	ID        string           `json:"id,omitempty"` // UUID for the message
    28→	Type      string           `json:"type"`         // MessageTypeUser or MessageTypeGemini
    29→	Content   string           `json:"content,omitempty"`
    30→	ToolCalls []GeminiToolCall `json:"toolCalls,omitempty"`

    37→	Args   map[string]interface{} `json:"args"`
    38→	Status string                 `json:"status,omitempty"`
    39→}
    40→
    41→// ParseTranscript parses raw JSON content into a transcript structure
    42→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) (*GeminiTranscript, error) {
    43→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
    44→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); err != nil {
    45→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    46→	}
    47→	return &transcript, nil
    48→}

     1→# Fix: Per-session agent resolution in multi-session checkpoints
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→A checkpoint can contain multiple sessions from different agents (e.g., session 0 from Claude, session 1 from Gemini). The per-session agent type **is stored correctly** in each session's `metadata.json` on `entire/checkpoints/v1` (`CommittedMetadata.Agent`), but the consumption layer collapses everything to a single agent — always session 0's. This means session 1's transcript gets written to the wrong directory, with the wrong file extension, and the wrong resume command.
     6→
     7→The user's key insight: `RestoreLogsOnly()` already reads each session's metadata (including `content.Metadata.Agent`) inside its loop — it just ignores it. Rather than threading `SessionAgents` arrays through `CheckpointInfo` → `RewindPoint` → consumers, we resolve per-session agents **at consumption time** inside `RestoreLogsOnly()` and return the per-session info so callers can print correct resume commands.
     8→
     9→## Changes
    10→
    11→### 1. Add `RestoredSession` struct
    12→
    13→**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — New struct returned by `RestoreLogsOnly`:
    14→```go
    15→type RestoredSession struct {
    16→    SessionID string
    17→    Agent     agent.AgentType
    18→    Prompt    string
    19→}
    20→```
    21→
    22→### 2. Change `RestoreLogsOnly` return type
    23→
    24→**`strategy/strategy.go`** — `LogsOnlyRestorer` interface (line 473-478):
    25→```go
    26→type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
    27→    RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error)
    28→}
    29→```
    30→
    31→**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Implementation signature (line 619):
    32→```go
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) ([]RestoredSession, error)
    34→```
    35→
    36→### 3. Per-session agent resolution in `RestoreLogsOnly()`
    37→
    38→**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Inside the session loop (after line 704), use `content.Metadata.Agent` to resolve a per-session agent:
    39→
    40→```go
    41→// Resolve per-session agent (fall back to outer ag for old checkpoints)
    42→sessionAgent := ag
    43→sessionAgentDir := sessionDir
    44→if content.Metadata.Agent != "" {
    45→    if perAg, err := ResolveAgentForRewind(content.Metadata.Agent); err == nil {
    46→        sessionAgent = perAg
    47→        if d, err := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot); err == nil {
    48→            sessionAgentDir = d
    49→        }
    50→    }
    51→}
    52→
    53→sessionFile := ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)
    54→```
    55→
    56→Collect `RestoredSession` entries and return them.
    57→
    58→Remove the outer single-agent `sessionDir` setup (lines 655-663) — each session computes its own dir.
    59→
    60→### 4. Per-session agent in `classifySessionsForRestore()`
    61→
    62→**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Same per-session resolution pattern:
    63→
    64→- Add `repoRoot string` parameter (caller at line 667 already has `repoRoot`)
    65→- Inside the loop, resolve per-session agent from `content.Metadata.Agent`
    66→- Use per-session agent + dir for `ResolveSessionFilePath`
    67→
    68→### 5. Update all callers in `rewind.go`
    69→
    70→5 call sites (lines 556, 605, 887, 928, 998) currently discard the return:
    71→```go
    72→if err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true); err != nil {
    73→```
    74→
    75→Change to capture `[]RestoredSession`:
    76→```go
    77→sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true)
    78→if err != nil {
    79→```
    80→
    81→Pass `sessions` to `printMultiSessionResumeCommands` (see step 7).
    82→
    83→### 6. Simplify `resume.go` caller
    84→
    85→**`resume.go`** (lines 408-486) currently:
    86→1. Calls `RestoreLogsOnly(point, force)`
    87→2. Re-opens the repository and re-reads the metadata tree to print resume commands
    88→
    89→With the new return type, eliminate the re-read — use `[]RestoredSession` directly:
    90→```go
    91→sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, force)
    92→if err != nil { ... }
    93→// Use sessions to print resume commands — no re-read needed
    94→```
    95→
    96→This removes ~40 lines of fallback/re-read logic (lines 422-486).
    97→
    98→### 7. Update `printMultiSessionResumeCommands()`
    99→
   100→**`rewind.go`** — Change signature from `(point RewindPoint, agent Agent)` to `(sessions []strategy.RestoredSession)`:
   101→
   102→For each session, resolve the agent from `session.Agent` via `ResolveAgentForRewind()`, then call `FormatResumeCommand`. Falls back to default agent for empty agent type.
   103→
   104→### 8. Unify `getAgent()` with `ResolveAgentForRewind()`
   105→
   106→**`rewind.go`** — Replace `getAgent()` body (line 33-42) to delegate:
   107→```go
   108→func getAgent(agentType agentpkg.AgentType) (agentpkg.Agent, error) {
   109→    return strategy.ResolveAgentForRewind(agentType)
   110→}
   111→```
   112→
   113→Fixes the inconsistency where `getAgent("")` errors but `ResolveAgentForRewind("")` falls back to Claude.
   114→
   115→## Files to modify
   116→
   117→| File | What |
   118→|------|------|
   119→| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go` | Change `LogsOnlyRestorer` interface return type |
   120→| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Add `RestoredSession`, per-session agent in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |
   121→| `cmd/entire/cli/rewind.go` | Update 5 callers, rewrite `printMultiSessionResumeCommands()`, unify `getAgent()` |
   122→| `cmd/entire/cli/resume.go` | Simplify caller to use returned `[]RestoredSession` |
   123→
   124→## Verification
   125→
   126→1. `go build ./...`
   127→2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`
   128→3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`
   129→4. Manual: create a checkpoint with two sessions from different agents, rewind, verify each transcript lands in the correct agent directory with correct resume commands
   130→

     2→// These are called by the hook registry in hook_registry.go.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (

    21→// ErrSessionSkipped is returned when a session should be skipped (e.g., due to concurrent warning).
    22→var ErrSessionSkipped = errors.New("session skipped")
    23→
    24→// handleGeminiSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Gemini CLI.
    25→func handleGeminiSessionStart() error {
    26→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
    27→}
    28→
    29→// handleGeminiSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Gemini CLI.
    30→// This fires when the user explicitly exits the session (via "exit" or "logout" commands).
    31→// It parses the session-end input and marks the session as ended so that subsequent
    32→// git hooks (e.g., post-commit) can trigger condensation for ended sessions.
    33→func handleGeminiSessionEnd() error { // Parse stdin once upfront — all subsequent steps use ctx.sessionID
    34→	ctx, err := parseGeminiSessionEnd()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		if errors.Is(err, ErrSessionSkipped) {
    37→			return nil
    38→		}
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session-end input: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	// Mark session as ended using the parsed session ID
    43→	if err := markSessionEnded(ctx.sessionID); err != nil {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	return nil
    48→}
    49→
    50→// geminiSessionContext holds parsed session data for Gemini commits.
    51→type geminiSessionContext struct {

    53→	transcriptPath string
    54→	sessionDir     string
    55→	sessionDirAbs  string
    56→	transcriptData []byte

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/gtrrz-victor/.claude/plans/tingly-shimmying-tiger.md",
  "content": "# Fix: Gemini session directory uses wrong hash during restore\n\n## Context\n\nWhen restoring Gemini session transcripts from checkpoints (`RestoreLogsOnly`), the file is written to the wrong directory. The actual Gemini session path is:\n\n```\n~/.gemini/tmp/<SHA256-project-hash>/chats/session-<date>T<HH>-<MM>-<shortid>.json\n```\n\nExample: `~/.REDACTED.json`\n\nThe `<SHA256-project-hash>` is stored in the Gemini transcript JSON as `projectHash`. But `GetSessionDir()` uses `SanitizePathForGemini()` which replaces non-alphanumeric chars with `-` — a completely different value.\n\n**During live sessions** this isn't a problem because `TranscriptPath` is captured from the hook input and stored in session state. But **during restore**, session state may not exist, so the code falls back to `GetSessionDir(repoRoot)` which computes the wrong directory.\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Add `ProjectHash` to `GeminiTranscript` and extraction helper\n\n**`agent/geminicli/transcript.go`** — Add field to existing struct:\n```go\ntype GeminiTranscript struct {\n    ProjectHash string          `json:\"projectHash,omitempty\"`\n    Messages    []GeminiMessage `json:\"messages\"`\n}\n```\n\nAdd extraction function:\n```go\nfunc ExtractProjectHash(data []byte) string {\n    transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)\n    if err != nil || transcript.ProjectHash == \"\" {\n        return \"\"\n    }\n    return transcript.ProjectHash\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Add optional `TranscriptAwareAgent` interface\n\n**`agent/agent.go`** (or a new small file `agent/transcript_aware.go`) — New optional interface:\n```go\n// TranscriptAwareAgent is implemented by agents that need transcript content\n// to determine the correct session directory at restore time.\n// Agents like Gemini use a projectHash from the transcript to compute the directory,\n// which cannot be reconstructed from the repo path alone.\ntype TranscriptAwareAgent interface {\n    GetSessionDirFromTranscript(transcript []byte) (string, error)\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Implement on `GeminiCLIAgent`\n\n**`agent/geminicli/gemini.go`** — New method:\n```go\nfunc (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDirFromTranscript(transcript []byte) (string, error) {\n    hash := ExtractProjectHash(transcript)\n    if hash == \"\" {\n        return \"\", errors.New(\"no projectHash found in transcript\")\n    }\n    homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n    if err != nil {\n        return \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get home directory: %w\", err)\n    }\n    return filepath.Join(homeDir, \".gemini\", \"tmp\", hash, \"chats\"), nil\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Use in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()`\n\n**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — After `GetSessionDir`, check for transcript-aware override:\n\nIn `RestoreLogsOnly()` (around line 707-712):\n```go\nsessionAgentDir, dirErr := sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)\nif dirErr != nil { ... }\n\n// Override with transcript-derived dir if agent supports it (e.g., Gemini projectHash)\nif resolver, ok := sessionAgent.(agent.TranscriptAwareAgent); ok && len(content.Transcript) > 0 {\n    if dir, err := resolver.GetSessionDirFromTranscript(content.Transcript); err == nil {\n        sessionAgentDir = dir\n    }\n}\n```\n\nSame pattern in `classifySessionsForRestore()` (around line 860-862).\n\n## Files to modify\n\n| File | What |\n|------|------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go` | Add `ProjectHash` field to `GeminiTranscript`, add `ExtractProjectHash()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go` | Add `TranscriptAwareAgent` optional interface |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` | Implement `GetSessionDirFromTranscript` on `GeminiCLIAgent` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Use `TranscriptAwareAgent` in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `go build ./...`\n2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`\n3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`\n4. Add unit test for `ExtractProjectHash()` in `agent/geminicli/transcript_test.go`\n5. Add unit test for `GetSessionDirFromTranscript()` in `agent/geminicli/gemini_test.go`\n6. Manual: create a checkpoint with a Gemini session, rewind, verify the transcript is written to `~/.gemini/tmp/<projectHash>/chats/` (not the sanitized path)\n"
}
```

  "file_path": "/Users/gtrrz-victor/.claude/plans/tingly-shimmying-tiger.md",
  "content": "# Fix: Gemini session directory uses wrong hash during restore\n\n## Context\n\nWhen restoring Gemini session transcripts from checkpoints, `GetSessionDir()` uses `SanitizePathForGemini()` (replaces non-alphanumeric chars with `-`) but Gemini actually uses a SHA256 `projectHash` stored in the transcript JSON. Add `extractGeminiProjectHash` helper\n\n**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Lightweight JSON extraction, no geminicli import needed:\n```go\nfunc extractGeminiProjectHash(transcript []byte) string {\n    var data struct {\n        ProjectHash string `json:\"projectHash\"`\n    }\n    if json.Unmarshal(transcript, &data) == nil {\n        return data.ProjectHash\n    }\n    return \"\"\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Override session dir in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()`\n\nAfter `sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)`, check for Gemini transcript hash:\n```go\nif sessionAgent.Type() == agent.AgentTypeGemini && len(content.Transcript) > 0 {\n    if hash := extractGeminiProjectHash(content.Transcript); hash != \"\" {\n        if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {\n            sessionAgentDir = filepath.Join(home, \".gemini\", \"tmp\", hash, \"chats\")\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Add `ProjectHash` to `GeminiTranscript`\n\n**`agent/geminicli/transcript.go`** — So the field is properly modeled:\n```go\ntype GeminiTranscript struct {\n    ProjectHash string          `json:\"projectHash,omitempty\"`\n    Messages    []GeminiMessage `json:\"messages\"`\n}\n```\n\n## Files to modify\n\n| File | What |\n|------|------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Add `extractGeminiProjectHash()`, use in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go` | Add `ProjectHash` field to `GeminiTranscript` |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `go build ./...`\n2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`\n3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`\n"
}

  "plan": "# Fix: Gemini session directory uses wrong hash during restore\n\n## Context\n\nWhen restoring Gemini session transcripts from checkpoints, `GetSessionDir()` uses `SanitizePathForGemini()` (replaces non-alphanumeric chars with `-`) but Gemini actually uses a SHA256 `projectHash` stored in the transcript JSON. During live sessions this isn't a problem (transcript path comes from hook input), but during restore the fallback computes the wrong directory.\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Add `extractGeminiProjectHash` helper\n\n**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Lightweight JSON extraction, no geminicli import needed:\n```go\nfunc extractGeminiProjectHash(transcript []byte) string {\n    var data struct {\n        ProjectHash string `json:\"projectHash\"`\n    }\n    if json.Unmarshal(transcript, &data) == nil {\n        return data.ProjectHash\n    }\n    return \"\"\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Override session dir in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()`\n\nAfter `sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)`, check for Gemini transcript hash:\n```go\nif sessionAgent.Type() == agent.AgentTypeGemini && len(content.Transcript) > 0 {\n    if hash := extractGeminiProjectHash(content.Transcript); hash != \"\" {\n        if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {\n            sessionAgentDir = filepath.Join(home, \".gemini\", \"tmp\", hash, \"chats\")\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Add `ProjectHash` to `GeminiTranscript`\n\n**`agent/geminicli/transcript.go`** — So the field is properly modeled:\n```go\ntype GeminiTranscript struct {\n    ProjectHash string          `json:\"projectHash,omitempty\"`\n    Messages    []GeminiMessage `json:\"messages\"`\n}\n```\n\n## Files to modify\n\n| File | What |\n|------|------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Add `extractGeminiProjectHash()`, use in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go` | Add `ProjectHash` field to `GeminiTranscript` |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `go build ./...`\n2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`\n3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`\n"
}
```

  "content": "# Fix: Gemini session path during restore\n\n## Context\n\nWhen restoring Gemini session transcripts from checkpoints, both the **directory** and **filename** are wrong:\n- **Directory**: `GetSessionDir()` uses `SanitizePathForGemini()` (replaces non-alphanumeric → `-`) but Gemini uses a SHA256 `projectHash` from the transcript JSON\n- **Filename**: Fallback creates `<sessionID>.json` but Gemini uses `session-<YYYY-MM-DDTHH-MM>-<shortid>.json`\n\nCorrect path: `~/.gemini/tmp/<projectHash>/chats/session-2026-02-10T23-57-9f5659bb.json`\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Add `extractGeminiProjectHash` helper\n\n**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`**:\n```go\nfunc extractGeminiProjectHash(transcript []byte) string {\n    var data struct {\n        ProjectHash string `json:\"projectHash\"`\n    }\n    if json.Unmarshal(transcript, &data) == nil {\n        return data.ProjectHash\n    }\n    return \"\"\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Add `geminiSessionFileName` helper\n\n**`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`**:\n```go\nfunc geminiSessionFileName(agentSessionID string, createdAt time.Time) string {\n    shortID := agentSessionID\n    if len(shortID) > 8 {\n        shortID = shortID[:8]\n    }\n    return fmt.Sprintf(\"session-%s-%s.json\", createdAt.Format(\"2006-01-02T15-04\"), shortID)\n}\n```\n\n### 3. Override path resolution for Gemini in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()`\n\nAfter `sessionAgent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)`, override both dir and filename for Gemini:\n\n```go\n// Override for Gemini: use projectHash for dir, session-<date>-<shortid>.json for filename\nif sessionAgent.Type() == agent.AgentTypeGemini && len(content.Transcript) > 0 {\n    if hash := extractGeminiProjectHash(content.Transcript); hash != \"\" {\n        if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {\n            sessionAgentDir = filepath.Join(home, \".gemini\", \"tmp\", hash, \"chats\")\n        }\n    }\n    agentSessionID := sessionAgent.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)\n    sessionFile = filepath.Join(sessionAgentDir, geminiSessionFileName(agentSessionID, content.Metadata.CreatedAt))\n} else {\n    sessionFile = ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, sessionAgent, sessionAgentDir)\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Add `ProjectHash` to `GeminiTranscript`\n\n**`agent/geminicli/transcript.go`**:\n```go\ntype GeminiTranscript struct {\n    ProjectHash string          `json:\"projectHash,omitempty\"`\n    Messages    []GeminiMessage `json:\"messages\"`\n}\n```\n\n## Files to modify\n\n| File | What |\n|------|------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Add `extractGeminiProjectHash()`, `geminiSessionFileName()`, use in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go` | Add `ProjectHash` field to `GeminiTranscript` |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `go build ./...`\n2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`\n3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`\n"
}

Add `ProjectHash` and `StartTime` to `GeminiTranscript`\n\n**`agent/geminicli/transcript.go`**:\n```go\ntype GeminiTranscript struct {\n    ProjectHash string          `json:\"projectHash,omitempty\"`\n    StartTime   string          `json:\"startTime,omitempty\"`\n    Messages    []GeminiMessage `json:\"messages\"`\n}\n```\n\n## Files to modify\n\n| File | What |\n|------|------|\n| `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` | Add `extractGeminiProjectHash()`, `geminiSessionFileName()`, use in `RestoreLogsOnly()` and `classifySessionsForRestore()` |\n| `cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go` | Add `ProjectHash` field to `GeminiTranscript` |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `go build ./...`\n2. `mise run fmt && mise run lint`\n3. `go test ./cmd/entire/cli/... -count=1`\n"
